### [44] [FREESS: A Web-Based Educational Simulator for a RISC-V-Inspired Superscalar Processor with Tomasulo-Style Dynamic Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30377)
*Roberto Giorgi,Miquel Moretó Planas*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: FREESS是一个开源的超标量处理器教学模拟器，提供RISC-V架构下指令级并行的周期级精确仿真，可视化展示Tomasulo风格机器的硬件状态演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了支持高级计算机架构课程教学，帮助学生理解超标量处理器中指令级并行的复杂机制，需要一个能够直观展示寄存器重命名、发射、执行、写回、提交和内存排序等硬件状态演化的教学工具。

Method: 设计并实现一个基于Tomasulo算法的超标量处理器模拟器，通过统一的文本表示展示寄存器映射表、空闲寄存器池、指令窗口、重排序缓冲区和加载/存储队列，支持周期级精确仿真，并允许动态调整发射宽度、队列大小和功能单元延迟等参数。

Result: 该工具已在高级计算机架构教学中使用约15年，并在GitHub上公开发布，成功帮助学生理解超标量处理器的硬件状态演化过程，并支持不同超标量组织结构的直接对比。

Conclusion: FREESS作为一个开源教学工具，有效支持了超标量处理器和指令级并行的教学，其可视化特性和可配置参数为计算机架构教育提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: FREESS (Free Educational Superscalar Simulator) is an open-source teaching environment for instruction-level parallelism in a RISC-V-inspired superscalar processor. It provides a compact, cycle-by-cycle view of register renaming, issue, execution, write-back, commit, and memory ordering in a Tomasulo-style machine. The simulator exposes the register map, free pool, instruction window, reorder buffer, and load/store queues in one textual representation, so the evolution of the hardware state can be followed on screen and reproduced on paper. Runtime parameters such as issue width, queue sizes, and functional-unit latencies can be changed easily, enabling direct comparison among alternative superscalar organizations. The tool has supported Advanced Computer Architecture teaching for about fifteen years and is publicly available on GitHub.

</details>


### [45] [Memory-Bound but Not Bandwidth-Limited: The Physical AI Inference Gap in Batch-1 LLM Decode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30571)
*Josef Chen*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 物理AI系统（机器人、自动驾驶等）的批量-1自回归解码任务虽是内存带宽受限，但更快的高带宽内存(HBM)并未带来成比例的延迟降低。在高端GPU（如H100）上，实际带宽利用率仅达峰值27%，主要瓶颈是kernel启动开销而非内存带宽。优化启动开销（如CUDA Graphs）在快速GPU上效果显著（H100提升1.259倍），但在慢速GPU上效果有限（L4仅1.028倍）。量化方法中仅GPTQ+ExLlamaV2接近理论4倍加速，其他方法未能充分利用内存节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理AI系统（机器人、自动驾驶车辆、具身智能体和边缘副驾驶）运行的是批量-1自回归解码任务，与云端LLM服务不同。这种工作负载通常被认为是内存带宽受限的，每个解码步骤需要流式传输模型权重和活跃的KV缓存。然而作者发现这种描述虽然正确但不完整：更快的内存并未带来成比例的延迟提升，需要深入探究缺失的因素。

Method: 在四款NVIDIA GPU（H100 SXM5、A100-80GB SXM4、L40S和L4）上测量三个7-8B级GQA transformer模型的批量-1解码性能。评估上下文长度从2048到16384，在受控bf16 SDPA设置下产生44个有效测试单元。通过CUDA Graphs进行A/B实验来隔离和测试启动侧开销的影响，并比较不同量化路径（bnb-nf4、AutoAWQ+Marlin、GPTQ+ExLlamaV2）的性能表现。

Result: 随着峰值HBM带宽提升，实际达到的峰值带宽占比反而下降：L4达到分析内存底线的约81%，而H100仅达到27%。CUDA Graphs在H100上将解码延迟提升1.259倍（95%置信区间1.253-1.267），而在L4上仅提升1.028倍。这证明了启动开销在快速GPU上成为可见瓶颈，在慢速带宽受限GPU上则被隐藏。量化路径中，bnb-nf4达到59.36 ms/步，AutoAWQ+Marlin达到45.24 ms/步（bf16基线62.32 ms），仅GPTQ+ExLlamaV2凭借Ada调优的int4内核达到17.36 ms/步，接近理论4倍加速。

Conclusion: 物理AI解码虽然受内存主导，但更快的内存并不能转化为成比例的延迟收益。核心发现是启动侧开销成为快速GPU上的关键瓶颈。部署意义在于：内存节省只有在运行时能够实现时才有价值。量化方法需要专门优化的内核才能充分实现理论加速，通用的量化路径无法完全恢复预期的内存流量减少收益。

Abstract: Physical AI systems, including robots, autonomous vehicles, embodied agents and edge copilots, often run a different inference workload from cloud LLM serving: single-stream, batch-1 autoregressive decode, where one robot, camera feed or user session waits on the next token. This workload is usually described as memory-bandwidth-bound. Each decode step streams model weights and the active KV cache, so latency should scale with peak HBM bandwidth. We show that this account is true but incomplete. We measure batch-1 decode for three 7 to 8B-class GQA transformers across four NVIDIA GPUs: H100 SXM5, A100-80GB SXM4, L40S and L4. We evaluate context lengths from 2048 to 16384, producing 44 valid cells under a controlled bf16 SDPA setup. The achieved fraction of peak HBM bandwidth falls as peak bandwidth rises. On the headline Qwen-2.5-7B ctx=2048 cell, an L4 reaches roughly 81 percent of its analytic memory floor, while an H100 reaches only 27 percent. Physical-AI decode is memory-dominated, but faster memory does not translate into proportional latency gains. We test the missing term with a CUDA Graphs A/B experiment. On H100 at ctx=2048, CUDA Graphs improves decode latency by 1.259x across N=10 fresh sessions, with a 95 percent bootstrap confidence interval of 1.253 to 1.267. On L4, the same intervention gives only 1.028x. This isolates a launch-side overhead that becomes visible on fast GPUs but remains mostly hidden on slower, bandwidth-bound GPUs. The deployment implication is that memory savings matter only when the runtime realises them. On L4, bf16 decode sits close to the memory floor, but common quantised paths do not recover the expected 4x weight-traffic reduction: bnb-nf4 reaches 59.36 ms/step and AutoAWQ+Marlin reaches 45.24 ms/step from a 62.32 ms bf16 baseline. GPTQ+ExLlamaV2, with Ada-tuned int4 kernels, reaches 17.36 ms/step.

</details>


### [46] [A Reconfigurable Computing In-Memory Macro with Charge-sharing-based Weighted Accumulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30814)
*Junyi Yang,Shuai Dong,Zhengnan Fu,Hongyang Shang,Arindam Basu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种支持多比特高度可重构的256x128 SRAM存内计算阵列，通过IMADC、BSCHA和双8T单元三项创新，有效解决了ADC开销大、多比特输入延迟高和读位线电压受限三大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SRAM模拟存内计算虽然效率出色，但面临三个关键挑战：显著的ADC开销、多比特输入的高延迟以及有限的读位线电压。这些问题限制了其性能和应用。

Method: 提出了一种支持1-7位输入、2-4位权重和1-7位输出的多比特高度可重构256x128存内计算阵列。引入三项关键创新：1) 仅占用3%面积开销的IMADC；2) BSCHA（位串行电流霍尔架构）；3) 集成读字线欠驱动级联技术的双8T位单元，实现三值权重存储。

Result: 实现了显著的性能提升：1) IMADC面积开销相比先前方案改善9倍；2) BSCHA延迟相比传统PWM和位切片模式分别降低1.9倍和6.6倍；3) 单元放电电流线性度改善7倍，可用读位线电压增加3.5倍。

Conclusion: 通过三项技术创新成功解决了SRAM模拟存内计算的核心瓶颈，在ADC开销、计算延迟和线性度方面实现了显著改进，为高效存内计算提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: SRAM-based analog computing-in-memory demonstrates outstanding efficiency. However, it faces three critical challenges: significant ADC overhead, high latency for multi-bit inputs, and limited read bitline voltage. To address these issues, this work proposes a multi-bit highly reconfigurable 256x128 in-memory computing array supporting 1-7b input, 2-4b weight, and 1-7b output. Three key innovations are introduced: 1) The IMADC occupies only 3% area overhead, achieving a 9x improvement compared to previous IMADC; 2) The BSCHA reduces latency by 1.9x and 6.6x compared to traditional pulse-width modulation (PWM) and bit-slicing modes, respectively; 3) A dual-8T bitcell enabling ternary weight storage through a decoupled read path, integrated with a read wordline under-driven cascode technique, improves linearity of unit discharge current by 7x and increases the usable read bitline voltage by 3.5x.

</details>


### [47] [HE^2: A Communication-Light Heterogeneous Architecture for Efficient Fully Homomorphic Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31004)
*Shangyi Shi,Husheng Han,Zhaoxuan Kan,Yinghao Yang,Jianan Mu,Tenghui Hua,Ge Yu,Xinyao Zheng,Ling Liang,Zidong Du,Xing Hu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了HE^2，一种通信轻量的异构FHE加速器，通过DFG优化和架构协同设计，实现了1.66倍加速和9.23倍更低的EDAP，解决了CKKS方案中异构通信瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CKKS全同态加密方案在隐私保护应用中具有潜力，但其计算密集型操作（ComOps）和内存密集型操作（MemOps）导致现有ASIC或NMP加速方案存在高硬件开销和有限效率问题。异构xPU-xMU架构虽能整合两种范式优势，但频繁且长延迟的异构通信（主要由keyswitch操作引起）成为关键性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出HE^2加速器，包含两个核心创新：(1) DFG级优化框架，通过识别并行keyswitch块并融合它们来充分利用hoisting算法的ModUp/ModDown归约潜力，从而降低通信频率；(2) 高效异构架构设计，采用组级流水线执行，利用分解组间的固有并行性有效隐藏通信延迟。

Result: 端到端评估显示，HE^2相比最先进加速器实现了1.66倍加速和9.23倍更低的EDAP（能量-延迟-面积积），通信停顿仅占总延迟的6.67%。

Conclusion: 通过DFG优化和异构架构协同设计，HE^2成功解决了CKKS全同态加密中的异构通信瓶颈问题，显著提升了性能和能效，为隐私保护应用提供了高效的FHE加速解决方案。

Abstract: CKKS, an emerging fully homomorphic encryption (FHE) scheme, has been promising in privacy-preserving applications by enabling SIMD fixed-point computations on ciphertexts. Despite its strong security guarantees, CKKS involves both compute-intensive operators (ComOps) with high computational cost and memory-intensive operators (MemOps) with large memory footprints, making existing ASIC-based or NMP-based acceleration approaches suffer from high hardware overhead and limited efficiency. This observation motivates the integration of the architectural advantages of both paradigms into a heterogeneous xPU (ASIC)-xMU (NMP) architecture. However, in such a design, frequent and long-latency heterogeneous communication caused by the dominant keyswitch operator remains a key performance bottleneck.
  In this paper, we propose $HE^2$, a communication-light xPU-xMU heterogeneous FHE accelerator with dataflow graph (DFG) optimization and architecture co-design. First, we observe that the majority of communication arises at the interface between ModUp/ModDown and neighboring MemOps. To address this, we propose a DFG-level optimization framework to fully exploit the ModUp/ModDown reduction potential of the hoisting algorithm by identifying parallel keyswitch blocks and fusing them for reduced communication frequency. Second, we design an efficient heterogeneous architecture that adopts a group-level pipelined execution to effectively hide communication latency by leveraging the inherent parallelism across decomposed groups. End-to-end evaluation results show that $HE^2$ achieves 1.66$\times$ speedup and 9.23$\times$ lower EDAP (Energy-Delay-Area Product) compared to the state-of-the-art accelerator, with communication stalls accounting for only 6.67% of the total latency.

</details>


### [48] [MixFP4: Enhancing NVFP4 with Adaptive FP4/INT4 Block Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31035)
*Jiaxiang Zou,Yonghao Chen,Ruilong Wu,Xinyu Chen*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出MixFP4，一种NVFP4的混合微格式扩展，通过在每个块中选择两种FP4微格式（E2M1和E1M2），在不增加元数据的情况下提升FP4量化的鲁棒性和精度，仅带来约3.1%面积和1.5%功耗开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型持续扩展，采用细粒度块缩放低精度格式（如NVFP4）因其显著的吞吐量和内存优势而日益普及。然而，单一FP4微格式往往与异构块级张量统计特性不匹配，导致量化性能下降。现有的NVFP4格式缺乏灵活性，无法适应不同块的数据分布特性。

Method: 提出MixFP4混合微格式扩展方案：1）在每个块级别动态选择两种FP4微格式（E2M1和E1M2）之一；2）复用NVFP4的缩放层级结构，通过重新利用FP8 E4M3块缩放的符号位来编码格式选择，实现零额外元数据开销；3）将两种微格式解码为统一的内部E2M2计算表示，避免数据通路重复；4）保持标准块缩放MMA/GEMM执行路径不变。

Result: 在代表性LLM家族上进行实验，MixFP4相比NVFP4基线在FP4量化鲁棒性和精度方面均有提升。硬件开销适度：张量核心面积开销约3.1%，功耗开销约1.5%。该方法在不改变执行路径且无需额外元数据存储的情况下实现了性能提升。

Conclusion: MixFP4成功解决了单一FP4格式与异构张量统计特性不匹配的问题，通过混合微格式机制在保持硬件效率的同时提升了量化性能。该方法仅需极小的硬件开销即可实现显著精度提升，为大语言模型的低精度量化提供了有效的解决方案，具有良好的实用价值和应用前景。

Abstract: As large language models continue to scale, fine-grained block-scaled low-precision formats such as NVFP4 are increasingly adopted for their substantial throughput and memory benefits. However, a single FP4 micro-format often mismatches heterogeneous block-level tensor statistics. To address this without changing the standard block-scaled MMA/GEMM execution path, we propose MixFP4, a mixed micro-format extension to NVFP4 that selects between two stored FP4 micro-formats (E2M1 and E1M2) per block. MixFP4 reuses NVFP4's scale hierarchy and encodes the format choice with zero additional metadata by repurposing the sign bit of the FP8 E4M3 block scale. By decoding both micro-formats into a unified internal E2M2 compute representation, MixFP4 avoids datapath duplication. Across representative LLM families, MixFP4 improves FP4 quantization robustness and accuracy over NVFP4 baselines with modest tensor-core overhead (3.1\% area, 1.5\% power).

</details>


### [49] [TRINE: A Token-Aware, Runtime-Adaptive FPGA Inference Engine for Multimodal AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22867)
*Hyunwoo Oh,Hanning Chen,Sanggeon Yun,Yang Ni,Suyeon Jang,Behnam Khaleghi,Fei Wen,Mohsen Imani*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: TRINE是一种单比特流FPGA加速器和编译器，通过统一层计算模式、运行时模式切换引擎、token剪枝和依赖感知层卸载技术，在嵌入式平台上实现高效端到端多模态推理，延迟相比RTX 4090降低22.57倍，能效显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 融合ViT、CNN、GNN和Transformer NLP的多模态堆栈在嵌入式平台上面临计算/内存模式差异大、硬实时目标余量小的挑战，传统方法难以高效执行此类多样化工作负载。

Method: 1) 将各层统一为DDMM/SDDMM/SpMM计算模式；2) 设计模式可切换引擎，在共享PE阵列上运行时切换权值/输出固定型脉动阵列、1xCS SIMD和可路由加法树(RADT)；3) 宽度匹配的两阶段top-k单元实现流内token剪枝；4) 依赖感知层卸载(DALO)在可重构处理单元间重叠独立核函数以维持利用率。

Result: 在Alveo U50和ZCU104上评估，TRINE相比RTX 4090延迟降低最高22.57倍，相比Jetson Orin Nano降低6.86倍(功耗20-21W)；token剪枝在ViT密集流水线上带来最高7.8倍提升，DALO贡献最高79%吞吐量提升；int8量化下各代表性任务精度下降<2.5%。

Conclusion: TRINE在单比特流中实现了统一视觉、语言和图工作负载的端到端多模态推理，达到了最先进的延迟和能效表现，为嵌入式多模态AI部署提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal stacks that mix ViTs, CNNs, GNNs, and transformer NLP strain embedded platforms because their compute/memory patterns diverge and hard real-time targets leave little slack. TRINE is a single-bitstream FPGA accelerator and compiler that executes end-to-end multimodal inference without reconfiguration. Layers are unified as DDMM/SDDMM/SpMM and mapped to a mode-switchable engine that toggles at runtime among weight/output-stationary systolic, 1xCS SIMD, and a routable adder tree (RADT) on a shared PE array. A width-matched, two-stage top-k unit enables in-stream token pruning, while dependency-aware layer offloading (DALO) overlaps independent kernels across reconfigurable processing units to sustain utilization. Evaluated on Alveo U50 and ZCU104, TRINE reduces latency by up to 22.57x vs. RTX 4090 and 6.86x vs. Jetson Orin Nano at 20-21 W; token pruning alone yields up to 7.8x on ViT-heavy pipelines, and DALO contributes up to 79% throughput improvement. With int8 quantization, accuracy drops remain <2.5% across representative tasks, delivering state-of-the-art latency and energy efficiency for unified vision, language, and graph workloads-in one bitstream.

</details>

### [50] [CREWS: Collaborative Robust Edge WiFi Sensing with Asynchronous and Incomplete Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30356)
*Yinan Chen,Yang Zhou,Xiaoxia Huang,Pan Li*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CREWS框架，通过拓扑无关聚合器和过期感知自适应回放机制，解决了协作WiFi感知中因网络波动导致的异步和不完整特征问题，在恶劣条件下准确率损失仅2.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的协作WiFi感知系统依赖于完美的节点同步和完整的数据可用性。然而，现实世界中的边缘部署面临异构计算和网络丢包问题，导致特征异步和不完整，影响系统性能。

Method: 提出CREWS鲁棒协作感知框架：1) 采用拓扑无关聚合器，对输入特征的到达顺序和子集大小具有不变性；2) 利用过期感知自适应回放机制，将滞后节点的过期特征视为系统诱导的困难样本，将同步延迟转化为有益的训练正则化。理论上证明了该架构的联合收敛性。

Result: 在8节点异构硬件测试平台上进行了广泛评估。在严重条件下（50%瞬态丢失率或分布外抖动），CREWS将准确率下降限制在仅2.2个百分点，显著优于现有最先进基线方法。

Conclusion: CREWS成功将网络波动下的同步延迟转化为训练优势，理论证明和实验验证了其收敛性和鲁棒性，为现实部署中的协作WiFi感知系统提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Existing collaborative WiFi sensing systems rely on perfect node synchronization and complete data availability. However, real-world edge deployments suffer from heterogeneous computing and network dropouts, leading to asynchronous and incomplete features. We propose CREWS, a robust collaborative sensing framework that inherently resists these network volatility. First, CREWS employs a topology-agnostic aggregator invariant to the arrival order and subset size of incoming features. Second, rather than discarding delayed observations, it utilizes a staleness-aware adaptive replay mechanism. By treating stale features from lagging nodes as system-induced hard samples, CREWS transforms synchronization delays into beneficial training regularization. We theoretically prove the joint convergence of this architecture and demonstrate how replay bounds the bias-variance trade-off. Extensive evaluations and an 8-node heterogeneous hardware testbed demonstrate its superior resilience. Under severe conditions i.e., 50\% transient dropout rate or out-of-distribution jitter, CREWS restricts accuracy degradation to merely 2.2 percentage points, substantially outperforming state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [51] [Emerging Trends in Intelligent Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30357)
*Ghazi Sarwat Syed*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了边缘计算如何通过在数据源直接执行计算来应对传统传感器架构面临的计算挑战，并分析了下一代智能传感器系统的关键架构和性能指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能、互联设备和高速移动网络的快速发展带来了前所未有的计算需求，这对传统传感器架构构成了挑战，促使研究者寻求新的解决方案。

Method: 本文分析探讨了边缘计算的转变趋势，即计算直接在数据源处执行，并研究定义下一代智能传感器系统的关键架构和性能指标。

Result: 本文识别并阐述了下一代智能传感器系统的关键架构和性能指标，为边缘计算在传感器系统中的应用提供了理论框架。

Conclusion: 边缘计算代表了传感器系统架构的重要演进方向，通过在数据源直接进行计算，能够有效应对日益增长的计算需求，未来智能传感器系统将围绕边缘计算架构和相应性能指标进行设计优化。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of artificial intelligence, connected devices, and high speed mobile networks is driving unprecedented computational demands that challenge traditional sensor architectures. This article explores the shift toward edge computing, where computation is performed directly at the data source, and highlights the key architectures and performance metrics that may define the next generation of intelligent sensor systems.

</details>


### [52] [Scheduling Mechanisms in Wireless Sensor-Actuator Networks for Multi-rate Periodic Control in Industry 4.0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30520)
*Dingwen Yuan,Luis F. Abanto-Leon,Matthias Hollick*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对工业4.0场景下无线传感器-执行器网络（WSANs）的实时调度框架，包含四种策略，显著提高了可靠性、可调度性，并降低了通信与存储开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业4.0环境中的多速率控制系统对无线传感器-执行器网络提出了严格的实时调度要求，需要同时满足高可靠性、高可调度性以及低通信与存储成本的需求。

Method: 提出了一个包含四种策略的新型框架：1) 两阶段调度以提高通信可靠性；2) 最小松弛度优先-剩余冲突（LLF-RC）算法，具有高可调度性和适中的执行时间，并能限制最大队列长度；3) 机会聚合策略进行高效的包聚合；4) 重复调度策略以最小化通信和存储成本。

Result: 仿真结果表明：机会聚合策略将可调度性提高高达97%，执行时间减少高达29%；重复调度策略的执行时间可忽略不计，将最大执行时间减少92%，最大通信和存储成本减少99%；LLF-RC算法在保持可调度性的同时将最大队列长度控制在适度水平。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架通过四种互补的策略有效解决了WSANs在工业4.0场景下的实时调度问题，在可靠性、可调度性、执行时间以及通信存储成本等多个维度上均取得了显著改进，适用于资源受限的设备。

Abstract: This paper investigates scheduling strategies for wireless sensor-actuator networks (WSANs) in Industry 4.0 scenarios. In particular, we address the problem of real-time scheduling for multi-rate control systems by proposing a novel framework. Our framework features four strategies that improve reliability, schedulability and execution time, and reduce communication and storage costs. Two-phase scheduling is our first strategy, devised to improve communication reliability. Our second strategy is the least-laxity-first with remaining conflicts (LLF-RC) scheduling algorithm, which has high schedulability and affordable execution time. LLF-RC also keeps the maximum queue length at a moderate level, making it suitable for storage-constrained devices. Our third and fourth strategies are opportunistic aggregation and repetitive scheduling. Opportunistic aggregation performs simple and effective packet aggregation, enhancing schedulability by up to 97% and reducing execution time by up to 29%, in our simulation. Repetitive scheduling has negligible execution time, and contributes to minimize communication and storage costs. It reduces the maximum execution time by 92% and the maximum communication and storage cost by 99%, in our simulation. We compare our proposed framework against existing approaches, and evaluate the advantages of our strategies in realistic scenarios.

</details>


### [53] [From Waves to Graphs: A Ray-Tracing-Inspired Neural Radio Propagation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30525)
*Paul Almasan,Stefanos Bakirtzis,José Suárez-Varela,Andra Lutu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了GRAPHWAVE，一种基于射线追踪原理的神经图驱动传播求解器，通过将传播环境转换为图表示并应用神经消息传递，实现了快速准确的三维无线电传播建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能驱动的无线电传播模型能够为移动网络运营商提供敏捷且稳健的解决方案，以确保无线生态系统的最佳性能并支持其高效扩展。现有的传播建模方法需要在精度与计算效率之间寻求更好的平衡。

Method: 提出GRAPHWAVE模型，利用传播环境的数字化版本构建点云，提取无线电环境的等效图表示；在等效图上应用神经消息传递机制，实现对三维环境中接收信号强度等无线电相关量的准确推断。模型可作为无线电环境的数字孪生使用。

Result: 该模型能够从合成数据和真实数据中学习，在保持低推理时间的同时，准确推断三维环境中的无线电传播特性（如接收信号强度）。

Conclusion: GRAPHWAVE成功将射线追踪原理与图神经网络相结合，为无线电传播建模提供了一种高效准确的解决方案，可作为无线电环境数字孪生应用于实际网络规划与优化场景。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence-driven radio propagation models provide agile and robust solutions for mobile network operators in their effort to ensure the optimal performance of the wireless ecosystem and support its efficient expansion. In this paper, we introduce GRAPHWAVE, a neural graph-driven propagation solver hinging on the governing principles of ray tracing. The proposed model leverages a digitized version of the propagation environment to build a point cloud and extract an equivalent graph representation of the radio environment. By applying neural message passing over the equivalent graph, it allows the model to accurately infer radio-related quantities, e.g., received signal strength, in a three-dimensional environment. We showcase the use of GRAPHWAVE as a radio environment digital twin and we demonstrate that the model can learn from synthetic and real-world data while achieving low inference times.

</details>


### [54] [Jamming-Resilient PRB Reservation for Latency-Critical O-RAN Network Slicing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30622)
*Elahe Delavari,Junaid Farooq*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对O-RAN架构下工业5G切片面临的对抗性干扰问题，提出了一种基于预留PRB的弹性资源分配框架，通过near-RT RIC xApp实现混合缓解策略，并利用掩码深度Q网络学习干扰下的最优控制策略，有效降低了URLLC业务时延违约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: O-RAN架构虽然支持近实时的软件化网络切片控制，但在工业5G下行系统中，对抗性干扰会骤然降低有效PRB容量，导致队列积压和持续的时延违约，尤其是在小区边缘用户频谱效率较低的情况下。现有的资源分配方法难以应对这种突发性的容量下降。

Method: 提出了基于预留资源的弹性框架：维护一个有限的预留PRB池，由near-RT RIC xApp控制；采用混合缓解策略，包括主动清除积压以构建时延裕度，以及在干扰激活期间反应性地分配预留容量；将预留激活建模为约束序列决策问题，设计掩码深度Q网络在非平稳干扰下学习有效的控制策略。

Result: 仿真结果显示，与反应式基准方法相比，该方法大幅降低了URLLC（超可靠低时延通信）时延违约次数，并提高了预留资源的使用效率。

Conclusion: 该预留式弹性框架能有效增强O-RAN切片部署在面对对抗性干扰时的鲁棒性，为工业5G系统提供了可行的时延保障解决方案。

Abstract: Open radio access network (O-RAN) architectures enable near real-time, software-driven control of network slicing through programmable xApps deployed on the near-real-time RAN Intelligent Controller (near-RT RIC). In industrial 5G downlink systems, adversarial jamming can abruptly reduce the effective physical resource block (PRB) capacity, triggering queue buildup and persistent latency violations, particularly in the presence of low spectral efficiency cell edge user equipments. This paper proposes a reserve-based resilience framework for PRB allocation in sliced O-RAN deployments. A finite pool of reserved PRBs is controlled by a near-RT RIC xApp that provides hybrid mitigation by proactively clearing backlog to build latency margin and reactively allocating reserve capacity during jammer active intervals. We formulate reserve activation as a constrained sequential decision problem and design a masked Deep Q-Network to learn effective control policies under non-stationary jamming. Simulation results show substantial reductions in URLLC latency violations and improved reserve efficiency compared to reactive baselines.

</details>


### [55] [Temporally Encoded Double DQN for Proactive PRB Allocation in O-RAN Enabled Industrial Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30630)
*Elahe Delavari,Xingqi Wu,Junaid Farooq*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种集成LSTM编码器的Double DQN深度强化学习xApp，用于O-RAN工业网络中的PRB主动分配，有效解决了非平稳条件下的调度问题，显著提升了切片满意度和缓冲区稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在智能制造业中，5G无线系统需要支持eMBB和URLLC等异构工业工作负载。然而，工业流量具有内在的过程驱动特性和时间相关性，O-RAN中传统的静态或响应式调度器在处理这种非平稳条件时表现不足，导致资源利用率低下以及无法满足时延-可靠性保障要求。

Method: 提出了一种时间感知的深度强化学习(DRL) xApp框架，用于O-RAN使能工业网络中的主动物理资源块(PRB)分配。该框架在Double Deep Q-Network (DQN)中集成了长短期记忆(LSTM)编码器，用于建模切片级关键性能指标(KPIs)之间的序列依赖关系，实现预测性和稳定的决策制定。同时引入连续时间马尔可夫链(CTMC)流量模型来模拟机器并发性和过程突发性。

Result: 实验结果表明，LSTM-Double DQN方法在中度和重度负载条件下能够改善切片满意度和缓冲区稳定性，其中最长的序列窗口提供了最强的性能提升。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了通过结合LSTM的序列建模能力和Double DQN的决策能力，可以有效解决工业网络环境中非平稳流量条件下的资源分配问题，为O-RAN架构下的智能资源管理提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Fifth-generation (5G) wireless systems are increasingly adopted in smart manufacturing to support heterogeneous industrial workloads through services such as enhanced Mobile Broadband (eMBB) and Ultra-Reliable Low-Latency Communication (URLLC). However, industrial traffic is inherently process-driven and temporally correlated. So, static or reactive schedulers in the Open Radio Access Network (O-RAN) are inadequate for such non-stationary conditions, leading to sub-optimal utilization and violation of latency-reliability guarantees. This paper proposes a temporal-aware deep reinforcement learning (DRL) xApp for proactive Physical Resource Block (PRB) allocation in O-RAN-enabled industrial networks. The proposed framework integrates a long short-term memory (LSTM) encoder within a Double Deep Q-Network (DQN) to model sequential dependencies among slice-level Key Performance Indicators (KPIs), enabling predictive and stable decision-making. A continuous-time Markov chain (CTMC) traffic model is incorporated to emulate machine concurrency and process burstiness. Experimental results show that the LSTM-Double DQN improves slice satisfaction, and buffer stability under moderate and heavy load, with the longest sequence window providing the strongest gains.

</details>


### [56] [Not All Roads Lead to Rome: How VPN Selection Alters What We Measure and Infer about Web Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30692)
*Sachin Kumar Singh,Robert Ricci,Alexander Gamero-Garrido*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本研究发现商业VPN不能作为国家内可互换的测量视点，不同VPN提供商会显著影响Web测量结果。测量变异性主要源于VPN提供商自建DNS基础设施、CDN基于出口网络的副本选择策略以及对等路由路径差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Web测量研究假设同一国家内的商业VPN是可互换的视点，认为任何VPN都能代表该国的网络状况。本文旨在验证这一假设是否成立，即不同VPN提供商是否会产生实质性的测量差异。

Method: 在14个国家使用4个主要VPN提供商进行大规模基于浏览器的测量，配合针对性的DNS和副本选择探测实验，从VPN到端点路径的三个层面分析变异性来源：视点身份、名称解析和副本选择。

Result: VPN提供商运营自有国内DNS基础设施，常拦截查询而忽略客户端配置；CDN基于出口网络进行引导，将相同查询发送至不同副本；对等路径将相同DNS响应路由至不同物理设施。这些底层因素共同导致测量结果存在显著变异性。

Conclusion: 商业VPN在同一国家内不可互换，测量变异性主要由客户端下层层级驱动。本文提炼出一套适用于VPN基础Web测量的报告实践规范，以提升测量的可重复性和结果的可解释性。

Abstract: Web-measurement studies treat commercial VPNs as interchangeable vantage points within a country, assuming that any VPN in a particular country is as good as any other. We show that this assumption does not hold: the same country measured through different VPN providers yields materially different conclusions about where endpoints sit, who hosts them, and which physical replicas serve them. Using large-scale browser-based measurements across fourteen countries and four major VPN providers, complemented by targeted DNS and replica-selection probes, we examine sources of this variability across three layers of the VPN-to-endpoint path: vantage identity, name resolution, and replica selection. We find that the variability is driven primarily by layers below the client: commercial VPN providers operate their own in-country DNS infrastructure, often intercepting queries regardless of client configuration; CDNs steer on the exit network, sending identical queries to different replicas; and peering paths route identical DNS answers to different physical facilities. We distill these findings into a set of reporting practices for VPN-based Web measurement.

</details>


### [57] [Where's Waldo Library? Using Reverse IP Geolocation to Identify Library IPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30791)
*Nishant Acharya,Anyu Yang,Humaira Fasih Ahmed Hashmi,Kevin Vermeulen,Shivani Kalamadi,Jiayi Liu,Ashutosh Kshirsagar,Elizabeth Belding,David Choffnes,Alexander Gamero-Garrido*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了反向IP地理定位框架（RG），用于从物理地址推断社区锚点机构（CAI）的IP地址，以支持大规模宽带测量和政策评估。以美国公共图书馆为研究对象，该方法结合多种数据源，约一半情况能成功映射图书馆的IP前缀，覆盖全美城乡地区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社区锚点机构（如图书馆、学校和社区中心）对为网络服务不足的个人和社区提供互联网接入至关重要，但许多机构自身网络资源不足。由于这些机构的IP地址未被系统记录，难以进行大规模的宽带质量和可靠性评估。因此，需要一种方法从公开的物理地址推断其IP地址。

Method: 提出反向IP地理定位（RG）框架，利用CAI街道地址公开的特点，从商业地理定位数据库识别可能服务于该位置的候选IP集合。方法整合了IP地理定位数据库、DNS PTR记录、WHOIS注册信息、宽带提供商数据和主动测量，以识别并验证分配给图书馆的IP地址。

Result: 以美国公共图书馆为研究对象进行验证，该方法约一半情况下能成功将图书馆映射到其IP前缀，覆盖美国所有州以及城市和农村地区，证明了在IP空间中映射CAI存在的可行性。

Conclusion: 本研究展示了从物理地址推断CAI机构IP地址的可行性，为大规模远程宽带基础设施评估奠定了基础，有助于改善对未充分服务社区的网络接入质量分析。

Abstract: Community anchor institutions (CAIs), such as libraries, schools, and community centers, are critical for providing Internet access to un- or under-served individuals and communities. Because many of these institutions are themselves under-provisioned, analyzing the reliability and quality of their Internet service is important. Doing so at scale requires knowing the IP addresses of these institutions so that broadband measurement and policy evaluation can occur. Unfortunately, these IPs are not systematically documented. As a first step towards widespread, scalable evaluation of CAI Internet connectivity, this paper presents Reverse IP Geolocation (RG), a new framework to infer IP addresses from physical address data. A key insight is that CAI street addresses are publicly known, which allows us to identify a candidate set of IPs from commercial geolocation that are likely serving the location associated with a CAI. In this paper, \textbf{we focus on US public libraries}, which offer both geographic diversity across thousands of locations, and some publicly available institutional records (\eg{}WHOIS registrations) that enable systematic validation of our approach. Our approach offers a novel integration of IP geolocation databases, DNS PTR records, WHOIS registrations, broadband provider data, and active measurements to identify IPs likely assigned to libraries and validate them. Based on evaluations, our approach can map a library to its IP prefix approx. half of the time, with coverage across all US states, as well as urban and rural areas. Our results highlight the feasibility of mapping CAI presence in IP space and offer a foundation for large-scale, remote broadband infrastructure evaluation.

</details>


### [58] [Stratifying the Digital Divide: Analysis of Socio-Economic Influences on Internet Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30809)
*Shivani Kalamadi,Aditya Bej,Sachin Kumar Singh,Varshika Srinivasavaradhan,Elizabeth Belding,Alexander Gamero-Garrido*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本研究通过分析1.7亿个速度测试数据，发现人口密度对互联网质量的影响是尺度伪象；在控制密度后，收入和种族构成是城市互联网质量差异的主要驱动因素，且互联网不平等具有地域特异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管技术不断进步，数字鸿沟仍是影响全球数百万人的紧迫问题。现有研究缺乏对城市内部互联网不平等的精细诊断，需要开发一个在人口普查街区层面诊断互联网不平等的框架，以识别其真实的社会经济驱动因素。

Method: 将约1.7亿个众包Ookla速度测试数据（2021-2025年）与美国人口普查人口统计数据配对，覆盖六个大都市区域。在量化和校正采样偏差后，使用随机森林回归和置换重要性方法识别下载速度、上传速度和延迟的社会经济驱动因素，并对区域进行密度分层分析。

Result: 人口密度在区域层面主导所有三个指标，但这种主导性是尺度伪象：在密度分层后，人口密度在中高密度社区的影响消失。控制密度后，收入和种族构成为主要驱动因素：收入持续主导上传速度，而种族构成对下载速度的预测能力强于收入或教育水平。

Conclusion: 互联网不平等具有地域特异性配置特征，不存在单一的国家叙事可以解释，有效的政策需要针对特定区域进行干预，而非一刀切的全国性解决方案。

Abstract: Despite numerous technological advancements, the digital divide remains a pressing issue affecting millions worldwide. We present a framework for diagnosing internet inequality at the Census Block Group level by pairing approximately 170 million crowdsourced Ookla speed tests (2021--2025) with U.S. Census demographics across six metropolitan regions. After quantifying and correcting for sampling bias, we use Random Forest regression with permutation importance to identify the socio-economic drivers of download speed, upload speed, and latency. Population density dominates all three metrics at the regional level, but this dominance is an artifact of scale: once areas are stratified into density bins, its influence vanishes in medium- and higher-density neighborhoods, revealing that socio-economic conditions are the true differentiators of internet quality in most urban settings. After controlling for density, income and racial composition emerge as the primary drivers, income consistently dictating upload speed and racial composition proving to be a stronger predictor of download speed than either income or education. Our findings demonstrate that internet inequality is locally configured: no single national narrative explains it, and effective policy demands region-specific intervention.

</details>


### [59] [Kairos: Lightweight Testing Framework for Timing-Induced Interaction Failures in LTE and 5G Core Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30985)
*Wei Guo,Yuanhao Li,Hao Zheng,Junman Qin,Jun Kong,Jiapeng Li,Qiang Fu,Jiadai Wang,Jiajia Liu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性研究了LTE和5G核心网中控制面交互的时序漏洞，提出了轻量级测试框架Kairos，在4个核心网中发现了20个新漏洞并复现了34个已知问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着蜂窝核心网向分布式和云原生架构演进，控制面交互变得日益复杂。研究人员发现在两个控制面交互之间引入特定时序会导致网络功能崩溃，将其定义为时序诱导的交互失败。然而，现有研究主要关注畸形输入和规范违规的识别，时序诱导的交互失败尚未被充分探索，这构成了本文的研究动机。

Method: 首先，建立了控制面交互模式的分类法，并分析了每种模式的失效模式；其次，设计并实现了Kairos——一个轻量级测试框架，该框架无需分析蜂窝标准文档即可暴露时序诱导的交互失败。该方法通过自动化测试来检测时序相关的漏洞。

Result: 在两个开源和两个商用LTE及5G核心网络上评估Kairos，发现了20个新的漏洞，并成功复现了34个现有问题。实验结果表明时序诱导的交互失败在LTE和5G核心网中普遍存在。

Conclusion: 时序诱导的交互失败是LTE和5G核心网中的普遍问题，应在未来的规范中予以明确考虑。该研究为蜂窝网络安全测试提供了新的视角和方法，强调了时序因素在网络安全中的重要性。

Abstract: As cellular core networks evolve toward distributed and cloud-native architectures, control-plane interactions become more intricate and bring new challenges. Among these challenges, we find that introducing specific timing between two control-plane interactions can cause network function crash, which we define as timing-induced interaction failures. Prior research primarily addresses identifying malformed inputs and specification violations, while timing-induced interaction failures remain largely unexplored. In this paper, we conduct a systematic study of timing-induced interaction failures in LTE and 5G core networks. First, we establish a taxonomy of control-plane interaction patterns and analyze the failure modes of each pattern. Then, we design and implement Kairos, a lightweight testing framework to expose timing-induced interaction failures without analyzing cellular standard documents. Evaluating Kairos on two open source and two commercial LTE and 5G core networks, we uncover 20 new vulnerabilities and reproduce 34 existing issues. Our results show that timing-induced interaction failures are prevalent in LTE and 5G core networks and should be explicitly considered in future specifications.

</details>


### [60] [HetCCL: Enabling Collective Communication For Mixed-Vendor Heterogeneous Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31000)
*Yuejie Wang,Tao Chang,Yuanyuan Zhao,Yulong Ao,Zeyu Gu,Zhiyu Li,Yanmin Jia,Yan Zhang,Mingjun Zhang,He Liu,Yongzhe He,Yonghua Lin,Guyue Liu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: HetCCL是一个支持异构集群的集合通信框架，通过高效的P2P传输和边界通信器机制，在异构设备间实现高达17-19倍的带宽提升，将LLM训练端到端速度提升最多16.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在异构集群上训练大语言模型时，现有的同构集合通信框架（如NCCL、RCCL）无法处理混合硬件环境，而支持异构的通信库（如Gloo、OpenMPI）在数据路径上存在严重的性能开销，亟需一种高效的异构集合通信解决方案。

Method: HetCCL提出三项核心技术：1）高效的异构P2P传输机制，消除主机-设备内存拷贝开销并将控制卸载到CPU；2）边界通信器机制，利用厂商集合通信库的内在归约操作实现厂商无关性；3）分层拓扑抽象，将集合通信分解为集群级原语，保证最优的跨集群数据传输量和带宽利用率。

Result: 在4种不同厂商支持和4种异构设置下的评估表明，HetCCL在异构通信中相比Gloo实现17-19倍的带宽提升，在端到端LLM训练中将每步时间加速最多16.9%。

Conclusion: HetCCL成功解决了异构集群中集合通信的性能瓶颈，为跨厂商硬件协同训练大语言模型提供了高效的通信基础设施，具有显著的实用价值和可扩展性。

Abstract: Training Large Language Models (LLMs) on heterogeneous clusters presents significant challenges for collective communication, as hardware from multiple vendors introduces diverse network and computational characteristics. Existing collective communication frameworks (e.g., NCCL, RCCL) designed for homogeneous environments fail to address mixed-hardware setups, while communication libraries with heterogeneous support (e.g., Gloo, OpenMPI) incur heavy overhead in the data path.
  This paper presents HetCCL, a framework that enables heterogeneous collective communication by efficient P2P transport across heterogeneous devices (e.g., GPUs), eliminating the host-device memory copy overhead while offloading the control to the CPUs. For combining collectives (e.g., AllReduce, ReduceScatter), HetCCL introduces a border-communicator mechanism that achieves vendor independence by using the intrinsic reduction in the combining collectives in vendor collective communication libraries. With efficient heterogeneous P2P transport and portable reduction mechanism, HetCCL proposes a hierarchical topology abstraction for heterogeneous clusters, dissecting collective communication into cluster-level primitives that guarantee optimal cross-cluster data transfer volume and optimal bandwidth utilization.
  We implement HetCCL with 4 different vendor support and evaluate it in 4 heterogeneous settings with benchmarks and end-to-end LLM tasks. Our evaluation shows that HetCCL achieves 17-19x higher bandwidth than Gloo in heterogeneous communications, and speeds up end-to-end training by up to 16.9% in the per-step-time.

</details>


### [61] [Offloading L7 Policies to the Kernel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31084)
*Laurin Brandner,Ayush Mishra,Sebastiano Miano,Aurojit Panda,Gianni Antichi,Laurent Vanbever*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了L7FP，一种基于eBPF的内核级服务网格快速路径，通过在内核空间直接执行应用层策略，将现实应用的中位请求延迟降低高达6倍，吞吐量提升3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务网格已成为部署微服务的事实标准，通过边车代理实现加密、路由和负载均衡等网络机制。然而，这种架构引入了显著的性能开销，因为数据路径上的代理导致了大量的上下文切换，严重影响了系统性能。

Method: 提出L7FP快速路径系统，根据高层策略自动合成基于eBPF的数据平面，直接在内核空间执行绝大多数应用层策略。该系统无需修改现有微服务代码，对不支持的策略可透明回退到传统服务代理（慢路径）。

Result: 完整实现了支持TLS和HTTP/2的L7FP系统。与最先进的服务网格相比，L7FP将现实应用的中位请求延迟降低高达6倍，同时吞吐量提升3倍。

Conclusion: L7FP证明了通过内核级eBPF加速服务网格的可行性，在保持透明性和兼容性的同时显著提升了性能，为服务网格的高性能部署提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Service meshes have recently emerged as the de-facto standard for deploying microservices. Conceptually, they provide a uniform abstraction for inter-process communication (IPC) between services by implementing common networking mechanisms -- such as encryption, routing, and load balancing -- and by allowing these mechanisms to be configured and composed through high-level policies. Supporting these policies, however, comes with a significant performance cost, since service meshes interpose proxies (``sidecars'') on the data path, leading to numerous context switches.
  This paper presents L7FP, a fast path for service meshes which can enforce the vast majority of application-layer policies seen in the wild directly in kernel space. Given high-level policies, L7FP automatically synthesizes an eBPF-based data plane which enforces them in the kernel. L7FP accelerates existing microservices without any code modification, and transparently falls back to existing service proxies (the slow path) for the few unsupported policies.
  We fully implemented L7FP, with support for both TLS and HTTP/2. Compared to state-of-the-art service meshes, L7FP reduces the median request latency of realistic applications by up to $6\times$ while sustaining $3\times$ more throughput.

</details>


### [62] [SQEEZ: Energy-efficient Location Sharing for Mobile Ad Hoc Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31339)
*Ram Ramanathan,Dmitrii Dugaev,Ryan Conyac,Alon Mor,Charlie Greenbacker*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SQEEZ机制，通过自适应抑制和压缩技术减少位置信息负载，在保证定位准确性的同时显著降低能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在公共安全、灾害救援和军事应用的移动自组网和网状网络中，周期性的网络范围节点位置数据分发对于共享态势感知和协作地图至关重要。关键挑战是在节点端以最小的能耗提供最大准确度的位置信息。

Method: SQEEZ机制结合了两种正交技术：（1）位置更新的自适应抑制；（2）更新数据包的时间和内联压缩。论文提出了新的度量标准Error-Penalized-Energy (EPE)，通过产生的误差来标准化能耗指标。

Result: 在30节点随机航点场景中，SQEEZ分别提高了EPE效率和可扩展性最多4.4倍和2.3倍；在9节点真实网络轨迹中将EPE效率提高了7.5倍。在高移动性场景下，压缩技术比抑制技术提供更大的改进，而在低移动性场景下则相反。

Conclusion: SQEEZ机制通过结合抑制和压缩技术，成功在能耗和位置精度之间实现了更好的权衡，显著提升了网络的EPE效率和可扩展性，为移动网络中的位置信息分发提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Periodic network-wide dissemination of node location data is crucial for shared situational awareness and collaborative mapping in mobile ad hoc and mesh networks for public safety, disaster relief, and military. A key challenge is to provide maximally accurate location information with minimal energy expenditure on part of the nodes. We present SQEEZ: a mechanism for reducing the Position Location Information (PLI) load that combines two orthogonal techniques: (1) adaptive suppression of location updates; and (2) temporal and inline compression of update packets. We describe the SQEEZ suppression and compression algorithms, analyze the tradeoff between location error and energy consumption, and introduce a new metric called \textit{Error-Penalized-Energy (EPE)} that normalizes the energy metric using the error incurred. Our simulation results show that, in the range of parameters studied, SQEEZ improves the EPE-efficiency and scalability in a 30-node random waypoint scenario by up to 4.4x and 2.3x respectively; and increases the EPE-efficiency by 7.5x in a 9-node real-world network trace. Compression provides larger improvements than suppression at high mobilities and vice-versa at low mobilities.

</details>

### [63] [Polynomial Histograms for Memory-Efficient Representation of Long-tailed System Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30360)
*Murray Stokely,Tim Hesterberg,Arif Merchant,Nate Coehlo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多项式直方图的方法，通过在每个桶中存储分布矩信息，在相同存储成本下比传统直方图保留了更多的信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式系统需要高效收集和聚合大量不同类型、维度的性能指标分布（如延迟分布），要求在最小化单个组件开销的同时，提供紧凑的存储表示以支持后续分析。

Method: 提出了一种用于分桶数据的信息损失度量标准，并引入多项式直方图技术——在直方图的每个桶中附加底层分布的矩（moments）。在相同存储成本下，对比了使用额外桶的传统直方图与带有矩注释的多项式直方图的信息保留能力。

Result: 在大型生产系统的文件系统指标数据集上进行了应用验证。理论分析表明，多项式直方图在特定条件下能以更低的成本提供更多的信息，优化了信息损失与存储开销之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 多项式直方图相比传统直方图，是一种更具成本效益的分布数据表示方法，特别适用于分布式系统中大规模多维性能指标的高效收集与分析场景。

Abstract: Distributed systems must frequently keep track of many different types of performance metrics across many different computers. For example, the latency distribution of certain operations may be computed for a large combination of computers, users, and operations. These empirical distributions need to be collected at minimal expense on the individual software components, efficiently aggregated across multiple dimensions, and stored in a compact representation for a variety of downstream data analysis applications.
  We describe an information loss metric for binned data that allows us to optimize cost of information loss from different histogram representations. We explore the use of polynomial histograms where each bin of a histogram is annotated with moments of the underlying distribution in that bin. These polynomial histograms are compared to traditional histograms using the same storage cost for additional bins instead of annotations in each bin. We describe an application of these techniques for file system metrics for a large production system, and analytically characterize when polynomial histograms offer more information at lower cost.

</details>


### [64] [Energy-Efficient Aggregation and Minimum-Degree Spanning Trees in Radio Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30546)
*Yi-Jun Chang,Yang Ze Guan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在同步多跳无线网络中进行聚合计算的随机分布式算法，能够在O(n polylog n)轮次内以O(Δ* polylog n)的能量复杂度完成，接近最优能量消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合任务在无线传感器网络中自然产生，节点通常由电池供电，无线活动是能耗的主要来源。由于能量资源有限且重要，最小化能量复杂度（即节点保持活跃的最大轮数）成为关键目标。

Method: 设计了一种随机分布式算法，在无碰撞检测的同步多跳无线网络中使用O(log n)比特消息。算法以高概率构建并执行聚合调度，同时构造一个最大度数与最优值Δ*相差O(log n)因子的生成树。

Result: 算法在O(n polylog n)轮次内以高概率完成聚合任务，能量复杂度为O(Δ* polylog n)，其中Δ*是网络图生成树的最小可能最大度数。作为副产品，算法还计算出一棵生成树，其最大度数在Δ*的O(log n)因子范围内。

Conclusion: 该算法的能量保证接近最优，因为对于任何聚合调度和任何图，都存在至少一个节点必须保持活跃至少Δ*轮，证明了所提算法的近似最优性。

Abstract: We study the aggregation problem in synchronous multi-hop radio networks with $O(\log n)$-bit messages and no collision detection. Each node initially holds a value, and the goal is to compute a global aggregate such as the sum of all values. Aggregation tasks arise naturally in wireless sensor networks, where nodes are often battery-powered and radio activity is the dominant source of energy consumption. Accordingly, our main objective is to minimize the energy complexity, defined as the maximum number of rounds in which any node is awake.
  Our main result is a randomized distributed algorithm that, with high probability, constructs and executes an aggregation schedule in $O(n \operatorname{polylog} n)$ rounds and using $O(Δ^\ast \operatorname{polylog} n)$ energy, where $Δ^\ast$ is the minimum possible maximum degree of a spanning tree of the network graph. This guarantee is nearly optimal: for any aggregation schedule and any graph, there exists a node that must be awake for at least $Δ^\ast$ rounds.
  As a by-product, the algorithm also computes a spanning tree whose maximum degree is within an $O(\log n)$ factor of $Δ^\ast$, with the same round and energy guarantees. For every tree edge, both endpoints learn that the edge belongs to the tree.

</details>


### [65] [A Datalog Framework for Conflict-Free Replicated Data Types](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31569)
*Elena Yanakieva,Annette Bieniusa,Stefania Dumbrava*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文首次系统地使用Datalog作为基础，构建了一个声明式框架，用于指定和推理无冲突复制数据类型（CRDT）及其应用程序的语义，并通过属性测试验证实现，通过协作图数据编辑案例研究评估了该方法的正确性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式应用越来越多地支持本地优先的共享数据协作，允许多用户在没有全局协调的情况下并发执行更新。这种协作需要精心设计以捕获并发交互的预期语义，而现有的CRDT设计和分析缺乏系统性的形式化方法。

Method: 提出一个基于Datalog的声明式框架，将CRDT语义建模为操作上下文上的可执行逻辑程序，使并发性显式化且具有组合性，从而便于自动化分析；利用属性测试比较实现。

Result: 通过协作图数据编辑案例研究评估了该框架，实验结果表明在正确性验证方面有效，并随着操作数量和副本数量的增加展示了良好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: Datalog可以作为原型设计和分析复杂CRDT及其组合的有效基础，为CRDT的设计、验证和分析提供了系统性的形式化方法。

Abstract: Distributed applications increasingly support local-first collaboration over shared data, allowing multiple users to perform updates concurrently without global coordination. Such collaboration requires careful design to capture the intended semantics of the concurrent interactions.
  We introduce a declarative framework for specifying and reasoning about the semantics of conflict-free replicated data types (CRDTs) and CRDT-based applications in Datalog. The framework models CRDT semantics as executable logic programs over operation contexts, making concurrency explicit and compositional, and thus amenable to automated analysis. As one application, we use property-based testing to compare implementations. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to systematically use Datalog as a foundation for prototyping and analyzing complex CRDTs and their compositions.
  We evaluate our methodology using a collaborative graph data editing case study and report experimentation results assessing correctness validation and scalability with an increasing number of operations and replicas.

</details>

### [66] [QASM-Eval: A Dataset to Train and Evaluate LLMs on OpenQASM-3 Beyond Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30358)
*Zhenxiao Fu,Lei Jiang,Fan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了QASM-Eval，这是首个专门用于训练和评估大语言模型在OpenQASM-3硬件级量子编程任务上的综合数据集，包含100个专家验证的测试任务和4000个训练任务，系统覆盖经典逻辑、时序调度、脉冲控制和复杂工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算仍处于NISQ时代，性能严重受噪声限制，需要硬件级能力（如中间电路测量、经典反馈、动态解耦和脉冲级波形访问）。虽然OpenQASM-3提供了这些硬件级编程接口，但缺乏专门的数据集来训练和评估LLM在此类硬件面向特性上的编程能力。

Method: 构建QASM-Eval数据集，包含专家验证的100个测试任务和4000个训练任务，系统覆盖经典逻辑、时序调度、脉冲控制和复杂现实工作流；设计扩展验证器通过语法检查、量子态验证和程序时间线验证来自动验证生成的程序。

Result: 评估显示当前最先进的LLM在OpenQASM-3编程任务上表现困难，但基于QASM-Eval的针对性微调能带来显著提升。数据集为硬件级量子编程提供了关键基准和训练基础。

Conclusion: QASM-Eval填补了OpenQASM-3硬件级编程数据集的空白，为加速开发可靠的NISQ时代硬件级量子编程LLM助手提供了重要的基准和训练基础。

Abstract: Quantum computing remains in the Noisy Intermediate-Scale Quantum (NISQ) era, where the performance is highly constrained to noise. Addressing the limitation often requires hardware-facing capabilities beyond gate-sequence circuit specification, including mid-circuit measurement and classical feedback for quantum error correction (QEC), precise timing control for dynamical decoupling (DD), and pulse-level waveform access for calibration. OpenQASM-3 was introduced to expose exactly these capabilities, providing a hardware-level programming interface. However, despite the rapid progress of large language models in code generation, there is still no dataset specifically designed to train and evaluate LLMs on OpenQASM-3 programs that involve its advanced hardware-oriented features. To address this gap, we introduce QASM-Eval, the first comprehensive dataset designed to train and evaluate LLMs on OpenQASM-3. Rather than focusing on quantum algorithm design or reasoning, QASM-Eval explicitly targets the language's hardware-facing features. QASM-Eval comprises an expert-verified test set of 100 tasks and a training set of 4,000 tasks, systematically covering classical logic, timing scheduling, pulse control, and complex real-world workflows. To automatically validate generated programs, we check syntax, quantum states and program timeline using an extended verifier. Our evaluation reveals that while state-of-the-art LLMs struggle heavily in OpenQASM-3 coding tasks, targeted fine-tuning on QASM-Eval yields significant gains. QASM-Eval provides a crucial benchmark and training foundation to accelerate the development of reliable LLM assistants for hardware-facing quantum programming in NISQ era. Data and code: https://github.com/fuzhenxiao/QASM-Eval

</details>


### [67] [Gait2Hip-60: A Unified Deep Learning Benchmark for Predicting Hip Muscle Forces and Joint Moments from Multi-Cadence Gait Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30374)
*Jiaqi Zhang,Ji Hou,Qing Sun,Xianzhi Gao,Bo Huo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究开发了基于深度学习的框架，可直接从下肢步态运动学预测髋部肌肉力和关节力矩。在健康受试者基准测试中，Transformer模型表现最佳（肌肉力预测R²=0.819，关节力矩预测R²=0.862），并在零样本外部验证中对股骨头坏死患者保持了中等预测能力（R²≈0.54-0.57），证明了该方法在临床快速评估中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的髋部肌肉力和关节力矩估计依赖于肌肉骨骼模拟，虽然信息丰富但耗时且难以在临床环境中推广应用。因此需要开发一种能够直接从步态运动学快速准确预测髋部动力学参数的方法，以促进临床应用。

Method: 研究收集了60名健康成年人在三种节拍器引导步频条件下的步态数据，以10个双侧下肢关节角度作为输入，以OpenSim推导的髋部肌肉力和髋关节力矩作为参考输出。在统一的受试者级别划分、预处理流程和评估指标下，训练并评估了LSTM、Transformer和Mamba三种序列模型。最佳模型（Transformer）随后在9名股骨头坏死患者的外部队列上进行了零样本直接测试。

Result: 在健康受试者基准测试中，Transformer在髋部肌肉力预测（RMSE=1.33 N/kg，MAE=0.57 N/kg，R²=0.819）和髋关节力矩预测（RMSE=0.11 Nm/kg，MAE=0.07 Nm/kg，R²=0.862）方面均达到最佳的受试者级别平均性能，在不同步行步频下表现相似。在零样本外部验证中，Transformer对ONFH患者的髋部肌肉力预测（RMSE=1.51 N/kg，MAE=0.70 N/kg，R²=0.537）和髋关节力矩预测（RMSE=0.17 Nm/kg，MAE=0.12 Nm/kg，R²=0.569）保持了中等预测能力。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持从步态运动学估计髋部动力学的可行性，确定了Transformer作为强有力的基准模型，并强调了在临床应用前需要进行更广泛的病理验证和改进泛化能力的必要性。

Abstract: Estimating hip muscle forces and joint moments during gait typically relies on musculoskeletal simulation, which is informative but time-consuming and difficult to apply in clinical settings. This study developed a deep learning framework to predict these hip dynamics parameters directly from lower-limb gait kinematics and compared three representative sequence models under a unified protocol. Gait data were collected from 60 healthy adults under three metronome-guided cadence conditions. Ten bilateral lower-limb joint angles were used as inputs, and OpenSim-derived hip muscle forces and hip joint moments were used as reference outputs. Three deep learning models of LSTM, Transformer, and Mamba were trained and evaluated using the same subject-level split, preprocessing pipeline, and metrics. The best model was then directly tested on an external cohort of 9 patients with osteonecrosis of the femoral head (ONFH) without retraining. In the healthy-subject benchmark, Transformer achieved the best subject-level mean performance for both hip muscle force prediction (RMSE = 1.33 N/kg, MAE = 0.57 N/kg, R2 = 0.819) and hip joint moment prediction (RMSE = 0.11 Nm/kg, MAE = 0.07 Nm/kg, R2 = 0.862), with similar advantages across walking cadences. In zero-shot external validation, Transformer retained moderate predictive ability in ONFH for hip muscle force prediction (RMSE = 1.51 N/kg, MAE = 0.70 N/kg, R2 = 0.537) and hip joint moment prediction (RMSE = 0.17 Nm/kg, MAE = 0.12 Nm/kg, R2 = 0.569). These findings support the feasibility of estimating hip dynamics from gait kinematics, identify Transformer as a strong baseline, and highlight the need for broader pathological validation and improved generalization before clinical application.

</details>


### [68] [Unicorn: Scaling High-Dimensional Time Series Forecasting via Universal Correlation Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30376)
*Haochen Yuan,Yichen Song,Yunbo Wang,Xiaokang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Unicorn框架，通过潜在原型码本解耦相关性建模与通道身份，解决了时间序列模型中通道独立性与通道依赖性之间的权衡问题，实现了跨异构数据集的可扩展预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列架构面临根本性权衡：通道独立模型虽能随数据量增加良好扩展，但忽略了关键的通道间依赖关系；而通道依赖模型虽具有强表达力，却受"维度限制"，难以在异构数据集间泛化。需要一种方法弥合这一差距。

Method: 引入Unicorn（通用相关性网络）框架，其核心是一个潜在原型码本，将相关性建模与特定通道身份解耦。通过将异构通道投影到共享的潜在空间，学习身份无关、可复用的交互模式，从而在具有不同维度和语义的域之间迁移。

Result: 大量实验表明，Unicorn显著优于最先进的预测架构，特别是在少样本迁移场景中表现突出。

Conclusion: Unicorn为多变量时间序列基础模型提供了一条可扩展的路径，成功解决了模型扩展性与通道依赖性建模之间的关键矛盾。

Abstract: Modern time series architectures face a fundamental trade-off: channel-independent models scale well with increasing data volume but ignore critical inter-channel dependencies, while channel-dependent models are expressive but remain ``dimension-bounded'', struggling to generalize across heterogeneous datasets.To bridge this gap, we introduce Unicorn (Universal Correlation Network), a framework for scalable, multi-dataset pretraining on high-dimensional time series. At the core of Unicorn is a latent prototype codebook that decouples correlation modeling from specific channel identities. By projecting heterogeneous channels into a shared latent space, UniCorN learns identity-agnostic, reusable interaction patterns that transfer across domains with diverse dimensionalities and semantics. Extensive experiments show that Unicorn significantly outperforms state-of-the-art forecasting architectures, particularly in few-shot transfer scenarios, offering a scalable path toward multivariate time series foundation models.

</details>


### [69] [When LLMs Learn to Be Consistently Wrong: A Multi-Model Study of Linear Representations of Synthetic Deception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30381)
*Vahideh Zolfaghari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究AI模型的欺骗对齐问题，发现线性探针可在模型浅层（1-3层）以接近完美精度检测合成不诚实行为，且该表征具有跨领域泛化性和抗噪鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欺骗对齐（模型保持准确内部表征却故意输出错误）是AI安全的中心挑战。研究旨在通过合成不诚实这一受控测试环境，系统研究学习欺骗的表征基础，而非直接研究长期风险更高的策略性欺骗。

Method: 提出多模型范式：对五种Transformer模型（Pythia-1.4B、Gemma-2-2B/9B、Qwen2.5-7B、Llama-3.1-8B）分别构建诚实和欺骗变体，使用LoRA在相同问题分布上微调；在均值池化隐藏状态上训练线性探针（逻辑回归）和MLP探针；进行机制分析，包括Fisher判别比、有效秩、质心几何、方向稳定性、跨领域对齐和校准（ECE）。

Result: 线性探针在四种架构的1-3层以AUC≥0.99检测不诚实（Pythia-1.4B峰值0.705）；逻辑回归探针匹配或优于MLP探针，支持线性表征假说；TruthfulQA训练的探针以近零损失泛化至MMLU；深层表征对高斯噪声鲁棒；发现两种机制：Pythia/Llama/Qwen表征坍缩 vs Gemma-2高维保留；不诚实方向在深层逐步巩固，最优校准ECE<0.01。

Conclusion: 鲁棒、领域不变的不诚实表征可通过适度监督微调快速确立，表明激活监测可能有效，但也揭示了欺骗表征在模型内部快速根植的特性，对AI安全监控具有重要启示。

Abstract: Deceptive alignment, in which models maintain accurate internal representations while deliberately producing false outputs, remains a central challenge in AI safety. While strategic deception is the primary long-term concern, synthetic dishonesty - induced via direct optimization on incorrect answers - provides a controlled testbed for studying the representational basis of learned deception. We introduce a multi-model paradigm in which honest and deceptive variants of five transformer models (Pythia-1.4B, Gemma-2-2B/9B, Qwen2.5-7B, Llama-3.1-8B) are fine-tuned using LoRA on the same question distribution. Linear probes trained on mean-pooled hidden states detect synthetic dishonesty with near-perfect AUC (greater than or equal to 0.99) as early as layers 1-3 in four architectures, while Pythia-1.4B reaches a peak of 0.705. Logistic regression probes consistently match or outperform MLP probes, supporting the Linear Representation Hypothesis. Probes trained on TruthfulQA generalize with near-zero loss (Delta AUC approx. 0) to held-out MMLU subjects. Late-layer representations show strong robustness to Gaussian noise, with Gemma-2 models exhibiting exceptional stability. Mechanistic analysis of Fisher Discriminant Ratio, effective rank, centroid geometry, directional stability, cross-domain alignment, and calibration (ECE) reveals two regimes: representational collapse in Pythia/Llama/Qwen versus high-dimensional preservation in Gemma-2. Across all models, the dishonesty direction consolidates progressively in deeper layers, with optimal calibration (ECE less than 0.01 except Pythia) achievable in layers 1-4. These results demonstrate that robust, domain-invariant dishonesty representations can be rapidly entrenched via modest supervised fine-tuning, with implications for activation-based monitoring.

</details>


### [70] [LLMs Without Deep Neural Networks: New Architecture, Benefits and Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30385)
*Vincent Granville*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 作者提出了一种替代DNN的新模型，该模型与RBF网络机制相似，但通过闭式解一步找到损失函数的全局最优，无需训练过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证作者提出的深度神经网络替代方案在LLMs背景下的有效性，该模型由中国研究人员关注的RBF网络启发，旨在提高可解释性和准确性。

Method: 介绍新模型的高层次概述，该模型基于与RBF网络相同的机制，但通过闭式解一步找到损失函数的全局最优，消除了传统的训练步骤。

Result: 通过案例研究和与类似方法的比较，展示了新模型的有效性，特别是在无需训练的情况下实现高准确性和可解释性。

Conclusion: 该新模型为DNN提供了一种有效的替代方案，具有更高的可解释性和准确性，且无需繁琐的训练过程。

Abstract: The purpose of this article is to provide validation to my deep neural network alternative in the context of LLMs. Very recently, there has been a significant interest by Chinese researchers in a model called RBF network, as a substitute to standard DNNs, with increased explainability and higher accuracy. It turns out that my new model, discovered independently, is based on the exact same machinery. But with a major twist: it does not need DNN as it finds the global optimum of the loss function in closed form, in one iteration, thus eliminating the tedious training step. Here I provide a high-level overview of my technology, with case study and comparison to similar methods.

</details>


### [71] [Reducing the GPU Memory Bottleneck with Lossless Compression for ML -- Extended](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30728)
*Aditya K Kamath,Arvind Krishnamurthy,Marco Canini,Simon Peter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了IBP（不变比特打包），一种面向机器学习的无损压缩算法，通过消除张量组中的不变比特来加速GPU与主机间的数据传输，在GNN训练、DLRM推理和LLM推理中分别实现了74%、180%和24%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习训练和推理过程中，数据集规模往往远超GPU显存容量，导致依赖PCIe进行按需张量传输，形成关键传输瓶颈。虽然有损压缩可缓解瓶颈，但会引入依赖工作负载的精度损失，在现有ML部署中使用复杂甚至不可行。因此，本文探索无损压缩作为替代方案以避免部署复杂性。

Method: 提出不变比特打包（IBP）算法：识别并消除张量组中的不变比特；设计GPU优化的解压缩机制，利用warp并行性、低开销位操作和异步PCIE传输；确定无损压缩在ML流水线中的最佳集成位置以最小化对GPU执行的干扰；提供易用API并集成到GNN训练、DLRM和LLM推理框架中。

Result: IBP平均实现：GNN训练加速74%，DLRM嵌入查找加速180%，LLM推理加速24%。成功在三个主流ML框架中展示了易用性和有效性。

Conclusion: 无损压缩是缓解ML系统中PCIe传输瓶颈的可行方案。IBP算法通过消除不变比特实现了高压缩率和高吞吐量，在保持无损特性的同时显著提升了ML工作负载性能，且易于集成到现有ML部署中。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) training and inference often process data sets far exceeding GPU memory capacity, forcing them to rely on PCIe for on-demand tensor transfers, causing critical transfer bottlenecks. Lossy compression has been proposed to relieve bottlenecks but introduces workload-dependent accuracy loss, making it complex or even prohibitive to use in existing ML deployments. We explore lossless compression as an alternative that avoids this deployment complexity. We identify where lossless compression can be integrated into ML pipelines while minimizing interference with GPU execution. Based on our findings, we introduce Invariant Bit Packing (IBP), a novel lossless compression algorithm designed to minimize data transfer time for ML. IBP identifies and eliminates invariant bits across groups of tensors, improving throughput through GPU-optimized decompression that leverages warp parallelism, low-overhead bit operations, and asynchronous PCIe transfers. We provide easy-to-use APIs, showcasing them by adding IBP support to GNN training, as well as DLRM and LLM inference frameworks. IBP achieves, on average, 74% faster GNN training, 180% faster DLRM embedding lookup, and 24% faster LLM inference.

</details>


### [72] [Functional MRI Time Series Generation via Wavelet-Based Image Transform and Spectral Flow Matching for Brain Disorder Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30387)
*Hwa Hui Tew,Junn Yong Loo,Fang Yu Leong,Julia K. Lau,Ding Fan,Hernando Ombao,Raphaël C. -W. Phan,Chee Pin Tan,Chee-Ming Ting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DSFM框架，通过小波变换和离散余弦变换的双重频谱表示，结合频谱流匹配模型，生成高质量fMRI数据，有效解决了数据稀缺问题并提升了下游脑网络分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 功能磁共振成像（fMRI）采集资源密集，高质量样本稀缺，限制了数据驱动的脑分析模型发展。现有生成模型难以复现原始BOLD信号的非平稳性、复杂的时空动态和生理变异性。

Method: 提出双重频谱流匹配（DSFM）框架：(1) 通过离散小波变换（DWT）将BOLD信号转换为小波分解图，捕获全局瞬态和多尺度变化；(2) 投影到离散余弦变换（DCT）空间，利用低频主导BOLD系数的局部能量紧凑性；(3) 训练频谱流匹配模型生成类别条件的余弦频率表示；(4) 通过逆DCT和逆DWT重建生理合理的时间域BOLD信号。

Result: 双重变换方法施加了结构化频率先验，保留了关键的大脑生理动力学特性，在下游基于fMRI的脑网络分类任务中展现了有效性。

Conclusion: DSFM框架成功解决了fMRI数据生成的关键挑战，通过频域变换和流匹配技术实现了生理合理且高质量的fMRI样本合成，为数据增强提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Functional Magnetic Resonance Imaging (fMRI) provides non-invasive access to dynamic brain activity by measuring blood oxygen level-dependent (BOLD) signals over time. However, the resource-intensive nature of fMRI acquisition limits the availability of high-fidelity samples required for data-driven brain analysis models. While modern generative models can synthesize fMRI data, they often remain challenging in replicating their inherent non-stationarity, intricate spatiotemporal dynamics, and physiological variations of raw BOLD signals. To address these challenges, we propose Dual-Spectral Flow Matching (DSFM), a novel fMRI generative framework that cascades dual frequency representation of BOLD signals with spectral flow matching. Specifically, our framework first converts BOLD signals into a wavelet decomposition map via a discrete wavelet transform (DWT) to capture globalized transient and multi-scale variations, and projects into the discrete cosine transform (DCT) space across brain regions and time to exploit localized energy compaction of low-frequency dominant BOLD coefficients. Subsequently, a spectral flow matching model is trained to generate class-conditioned cosine-frequency representation. The generated samples are reconstructed through inverse DCT and inverse DWT operations to recover physiologically plausible time-domain BOLD signals. This dual-transform approach imposes structured frequency priors and preserves key physiological brain dynamics. Ultimately, we demonstrate the efficacy of our approach through improved downstream fMRI-based brain network classification. The code is available at https://github.com/htew0001/DSFM.git .

</details>


### [73] [Federated Variational Preference Alignment with Gumbel-Softmax Prior for Personalized User Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30873)
*Jabin Koo,Hoyoung Kim,Minwoo Jang,Jungseul Ok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedVPA-GP框架，通过联邦混合先验和正交损失解决联邦学习中用户偏好冲突问题，实现了多样化的偏好解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习框架在对齐大语言模型时通常采用单一奖励模型，这会不可避免地平均化相互冲突的用户偏好（如有用性与无害性）。虽然变分偏好学习（VPL）提供了个性化路径，但将其适应到去中心化设置面临严峻挑战：由局部数据稀缺和异质性导致的后验坍塌问题。

Method: 提出联邦变分偏好对齐框架FedVPA-GP：1）引入联邦混合先验，使客户端能够利用聚合的总体分布作为动态先验，从而稳定变分推理；2）设计正交损失函数，在潜在空间中显式强制偏好原型的分离。该框架在保护隐私的前提下解耦多样化偏好。

Result: 在HH-RLHF数据集上的实验表明，FedVPA-GP显著优于单一模型基线，成功解耦了相互冲突的用户意图，并实现了动态偏好切换。

Conclusion: FedVPA-GP框架有效解决了联邦学习环境下用户偏好对齐中的偏好冲突问题，为个性化大语言模型对齐提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) offers a privacy-preserving pathway for aligning Large Language Models (LLMs); however, existing frameworks typically enforce a monolithic reward model, inevitably averaging out inherently conflicting user preferences (e.g., helpfulness vs. harmlessness). While Variational Preference Learning (VPL) offers a pathway to personalization, adapting it to decentralized settings presents a fundamental challenge: posterior collapse driven by severe local data scarcity and heterogeneity. In this paper, we propose Federated Variational Preference Alignment with Gumbel-Softmax Prior (FedVPA-GP), a framework designed to disentangle diverse preferences without compromising privacy. To stabilize variational inference, we introduce a Federated Mixture Prior that enables clients to leverage the aggregate population distribution as a dynamic prior. Furthermore, we incorporate an Orthogonal Loss that explicitly enforces the separation of preference prototypes in the latent space. Experiments on the HH-RLHF dataset demonstrate that FedVPA-GP significantly outperforms monolithic baselines, successfully disentangling conflicting user intents and enabling dynamic preference switching.

</details>


### [74] [A Novel Evaluation Metric for Unsupervised Learning in AIS-Based Maritime Anomaly Detection: MADQI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30388)
*Ismet Gocer,Zakirul Bhuiyan,Raza Hasan,Shakeel Ahmad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为MADQI的新型质量评估框架，用于在海事AIS数据集中进行无监督异常检测的系统化评估，无需标签数据即可有效评估模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无监督异常检测算法（如孤立森林）在海事AIS数据异常检测中缺乏系统性和有意义的评估指标，因此需要一种能够在无标签情况下评估模型性能的新型质量度量方法。

Method: 提出Maritime Anomaly Detection Quality Index (MADQI)复合指标，整合四个相互关联的度量：异常率一致性(ARC)、物理合理性得分(PPS)、分数分布分离度(SDS)和极端案例证据(ECE)。框架使用Haversine距离计算分析AIS数据集，通过多块评估和自适应缩放技术实现自动归一化。

Result: 在AIS数据集上的实验显示，该框架实现了80.37%的MADQI得分。其中ECE和ARC分别达到0.907和1.000的分数，表明在检测极端异常和维持异常率一致性方面具有优异能力。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明该框架为海事AIS数据中的无监督异常检测评估提供了可靠且有意义的方法，特别在识别异常船舶行为方面表现强劲，证明了其有效性和实用价值。

Abstract: This paper introduces a new systematic framework for detecting anomalies in maritime Automatic Identification System (AIS) datasets. These anomalies include abnormal vessel behaviours related to speed, position jumps, time gaps, and turn angles. Although unsupervised learning algorithms such as Isolation Forest are widely used for detecting anomalous vessel movements, they often lack systematic and meaningful evaluation measures. To address this limitation, we propose a novel quality metric called Maritime Anomaly Detection Quality Index (MADQI). The prosed MADQI is a composite index designed to evaluate the anomaly detection performance of machine learning models without requiring labelled data. The proposed framework uses Haversine distance calculations to analyse AIS datasets and identify anomalies based on their spatial and behavioural characteristics. The proposed MADQI evaluation framework integrates four interconnected metrics: Anomaly Rate Consistency (ARC), Physical Plausibility Score (PPS), Score Distribution Separation (SDS), and Extreme Case Evidence (ECE). These metrics are combined through automatic normalisation using multi-chunk evaluation and adaptive scaling techniques. Experimental results on the AIS dataset show that the proposed framework achieved a MADQI score of 80.37%, demonstrating its effectiveness for unsupervised anomaly detection. In particular, the algorithm performed strongly in identifying abnormal vessel behaviour. Among the individual MADQI components, ECE and ARC achieved scores of 0.907 and 1.000, respectively, indicating excellent capability in detecting extreme anomalies and maintaining anomaly rate consistency. Overall, these results are encouraging and demonstrate that the proposed framework provides a reliable and meaningful approach for evaluating unsupervised anomaly detection in maritime AIS data.

</details>


### [75] [LongDS-Bench: On the Failure of Long-Horizon Agentic Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30434)
*Kewei Xu,Xiaoben Lu,Shuofei Qiao,Zihan Ding,Haoming Xu,Lei Liang,Ningyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了LongDS，一个用于评估长视野、多轮数据分析的基准测试，包含68个来自真实Kaggle笔记本的任务。实验显示当前最优模型平均准确率仅为48.45%，且52%–69%的失败源于长视野错误，揭示了智能体在维护演化分析状态方面的关键瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的数据分析具有内在的迭代特性，但现有基准主要评估孤立的或短交互任务，缺乏对智能体在长视野下跟踪演化分析上下文能力的测试。

Method: 构建LongDS基准，包含68个源自真实Kaggle笔记本的任务，跨越六个领域（如地球科学、商业和教育），涵盖2,225个轮次。任务设计围绕状态演化模式（如反事实扰动、回滚、多状态组合），平均依赖跨度为11.3轮。对五个前沿模型进行系统评估。

Result: 最佳模型平均准确率仅为48.45%；从早期轮次到晚期轮次，性能下降近47个百分点；52%–69%的失败归因于长视野错误。进一步分析表明，增加智能体交互步骤并不一定能提升性能，关键瓶颈在于正确维护分析状态。

Conclusion: 当前智能体在长视野数据分析任务中的核心挑战是正确维护和演化分析状态，而非交互预算不足。LongDS为可靠的长视野智能数据分析研究提供了重要基准。

Abstract: Real-world data analysis is inherently iterative, yet existing benchmarks mostly evaluate isolated or short interactive tasks, leaving agents' ability to track evolving analytical context over long horizons untested. We introduce LongDS, a benchmark for long-horizon, multi-turn data analysis where agents must maintain, update, restore, and compose evolving analytical states. LongDS comprises 68 tasks constructed from real-world Kaggle notebooks, spanning 2,225 turns across six domains including Geoscience, Business, and Education. Tasks are designed around state-evolution patterns (e.g., counterfactual perturbation, rollback, multi-state composition), with an average dependency span of 11.3 turns. Evaluating five state-of-the-art models, we find that the best model reaches only 48.45% average accuracy, performance drops nearly 47 points from early to late turns, and long-horizon errors account for 52%--69% of failures. Further analysis shows that additional agent steps do not necessarily improve performance, suggesting that the key bottleneck is maintaining a correct analytical state rather than increasing interaction budget. We release LongDS to support research on reliable long-horizon agentic data analysis. Code and data will be released at https://github.com/zjunlp/DataMind.

</details>


### [76] [DG-CoLearn: An Efficient Collaborative Learning Framework for Dynamic Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31427)
*Ashley Hoi-Ting Au,Zikun Zhang,Ligang He,Qiang Ni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了DG-CoLearn，一种客户端不可知的协作式动态图学习框架，通过增量图快照处理实现了高达33.8倍的训练加速和显著的性能提升，同时保护了跨客户端的结构隐私。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的动态图学习方法由于需要对完整快照进行重复再训练而存在巨大的计算开销，且不适合数据分区的协作学习场景。此外，现实图系统中跨分区边不可避免，但直接共享图结构会违反隐私约束，因此需要一种既能保证效率又能保护隐私的协作式动态图学习方案。

Method: 提出DG-CoLearn框架，采用基于增量图快照处理的客户端不可知协作动态图学习方法。该方法将计算集中在受时间更新影响的图区域，通过时间建模保留历史信息。在整个图处理流程中应用增量设计，包括服务器介导的嵌入交换机制，实现在不暴露原始跨客户端结构信息的情况下进行准确的多跳消息传递。

Result: 实验结果表明，DG-CoLearn在训练时间上实现了高达33.8倍的加速，通信开销减少27.4倍，同时在节点分类任务上F1分数提升高达13.36%，在链路预测任务上MAP提升高达8.27%。

Conclusion: DG-CoLearn成功地在协作式动态图学习中实现了效率、可扩展性和客户端间结构隐私保护的有效平衡，为解决现实场景中的动态图学习挑战提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Dynamic graph learning (DGL) is essential for modelling evolving graph data, but existing methods suffer from significant computational overhead due to repeated full-snapshot retraining and are not well-suited for collaborative settings with partitioned data. In realistic graph systems, cross-partition edges are unavoidable, but direct sharing of graph structure between clients may violate privacy constraints. We propose DG-CoLearn, a client-oblivious collaborative dynamic graph learning framework built on incremental graph snapshot processing, which focuses computation on graph regions affected by temporal updates while preserving historical information through temporal modelling. This incremental design is consistently applied across the entire graph processing pipeline, including a server-mediated embedding exchange mechanism to enable accurate multi-hop message passing without exposing raw cross-client structural information. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DG-CoLearn achieves up to 33.8$\times$ speedup in training time and 27.4$\times$ reduction in communication overhead, while consistently improving predictive performance on both node classification (up to 13.36% F1 improvement) and link prediction (up to 8.27% MAP improvement) tasks. These results highlight the effectiveness of DG-CoLearn in bridging efficiency, scalability, and client-to-client structural privacy in collaborative dynamic graph learning.

</details>


### [77] [NumLeak: Public Numeric Benchmarks as Latent Labels in Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30393)
*Anany Kotawala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了顶级大语言模型可能通过记忆训练数据而非真正理解来回答数值型基准问题，提出NumLeak框架量化了这种记忆泄漏，并发现简单的系统提示可有效防御非自适应攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共数值基准数据可能出现在模型预训练中，导致基于日期条件的评估可能测量的是记忆回溯能力而非样本外技能。需要区分模型是真正理解还是单纯记忆了这些数值数据。

Method: 提出NumLeak测量框架，结合生产模型的API边界探测和开放因果语言模型的白盒控制验证。使用对数概率排名检测开放式生成遗漏的记忆现象，并通过残差化方法隔离模型的记忆成分。

Result: 顶级前沿LLM在Fama-French市场超额回报上达到3-seed池化Pearson相关系数r=0.97-0.99，在五个兄弟因子上保持在0.15的25个基点内，类似保真度也出现在美国失业率、CPI通胀和NOAA温度数据上。在新发布的保留数据上，解析率降至21-57%但已回答月份的r仍保持在约0.99，表现出记忆通道预测的拒绝-回溯不对称性。Sonnet的"日期到市场情绪"回归与真实Mkt-RF相关r=0.74，在残差化后降至r=0.02。

Conclusion: 闭源API黑盒探测低估了记忆泄漏渠道，因为对数概率排名能检测到开放式生成遗漏的记忆现象。简单的一行系统提示防御以近乎零的效用成本阻止了99.8%的非自适应单轮后缀攻击集。

Abstract: Public numeric benchmarks appear in pretraining, so an evaluation that conditions on a date may be measuring memorized recall rather than out-of-sample skill. We introduce NumLeak, a measurement framework that combines API-boundary probes on production models with a white-box controlled validation on an open causal LM. Top-tier frontier LLMs recall the Fama-French market excess return at 3-seed pooled Pearson r=0.97-0.99 while staying within 0.15 within-25bps on the five sibling factors; comparable fidelity appears on U.S. unemployment, CPI inflation, and NOAA temperature. On a recent-release holdout, parse rate collapses to 21-57% but r stays at approximately 0.99 on months answered, the refuse-or-recall asymmetry a memorized channel predicts. The white-box experiment reproduces the dose-response, and logprob ranking detects memorization that open-ended generation misses, implying closed-API black-box probes understate the channel. A Sonnet "date to market-sentiment" regression that correlates with true Mkt-RF at r=0.74 collapses to r=0.02 once the model's own recall is residualized out. A one-line system-prompt defense blocks 99.8% of a non-adaptive single-turn suffix attack set at near-zero utility cost on conceptual and historical-narrative queries

</details>


### [78] [PithTrain: A Compact and Agent-Native MoE Training System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31463)
*Ruihang Lai,Hao Kang,Haozhan Tang,Akaash R. Parthasarathy,Zichun Yu,Junru Shao,Todd C. Mowry,Chenyan Xiong,Tianqi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PithTrain，一个面向AI编程代理优化的MoE训练框架，在保持与生产框架相同吞吐量的同时，将代理交互轮次减少62%，GPU活跃时间减少64%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生产级MoE训练框架虽经多年工程优化，但对新架构和系统优化的适配成本高昂。AI编码代理可自动化部分开发流程，但现有框架对代理不够友好，存在未被现有吞吐量评估覆盖的隐性成本——代理任务效率（Agent-Task Efficiency, ATE），即代理理解、操作和扩展框架的成本。

Method: 基于四项代理原生设计原则构建PithTrain紧凑型代理原生MoE训练框架；引入ATE-Bench基准测试，涵盖真实世界的训练框架任务，用于评估代理任务效率。

Result: PithTrain在生产吞吐量上与现有生产框架持平；在ATE-Bench评估中，PithTrain显著提升代理任务效率，代理交互轮次（Agent Turns）减少高达62%，活跃GPU时间减少64%。

Conclusion: 通过代理原生设计，可在不牺牲训练吞吐量的前提下大幅降低AI编码代理的使用成本，加速训练框架的演进，证明了将代理任务效率作为框架设计核心维度的重要性。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) has become the dominant architecture for frontier language models. To meet this demand, production frameworks have built optimized MoE training stacks over years of engineering effort. Yet evolving these stacks for new architectures and system optimizations remains expensive. With the rise of AI coding agents, they could automate parts of training-framework development and accelerate this evolution. But applying them to these existing frameworks carries hidden costs, invisible to today's throughput-only evaluations. We name this missing dimension agent-task efficiency (ATE): the cost of using coding agents to understand, operate, and extend a framework. Grounded in four agent-native design principles, we build PithTrain, a compact, agent-native MoE training framework. We further introduce ATE-Bench, covering real-world training-framework tasks. Our evaluation shows PithTrain matches the throughput of production frameworks, and on ATE-Bench, PithTrain enables higher agent-task efficiency, with up to 62% fewer Agent Turns and 64% less Active GPU Time.

</details>


### [79] [Calibrated Preference Learning: The Case of Label Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30447)
*Santo M. A. R. Thies,Viktor Bengs,Timo Kaufmann,Sebastian J. Vollmer,Eyke Hüllermeier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次形式化了概率标签排序任务的校准问题，提出了覆盖完整排序、子排序和top-k排序的层次化校准概念，证明了理论关系，并发现现有模型普遍存在校准不良的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 校准对于可靠决策至关重要，已在分类和回归任务中被广泛研究，但在概率标签排序任务中尚未被正式解决。朴素地将排序视为类别会忽略其结构，且无法捕获成对和top-k预测等重要模态。

Method: 形式化标签排序的校准定义，建立覆盖完整排序、子排序和top-k排序的层次化校准概念体系；理论证明这些概念之间的关系；在流行标签排序模型和RLHF奖励模型上进行实证分析。

Result: 证明了完整排序校准蕴含其他校准，但反之不成立；子排序校准和top-k校准不可比较。实证发现流行标签排序模型普遍校准不良，子排序和top-k指标存在显著差异。在RLHF奖励模型上，校准与基准准确率强相关但不完全相关。

Conclusion: 校准捕获了超越top-1准确率的有意义质量维度，这些发现为理解校准不良的下游影响和开发校正方法提供了动力。

Abstract: Calibration, the alignment of predicted probabilities with true outcome frequencies, is essential for reliable decision-making. While extensively studied for classification and regression, calibration has not been formally addressed for probabilistic label ranking, where the goal is to predict a distribution over orderings of a label set. Naively treating rankings as classes ignores their structure and fails to capture important modalities such as pairwise and top-k predictions. We formalize calibration for label ranking and develop a hierarchy of notions covering full rankings, sub-rankings, and top-k rankings. We prove that full-rank calibration implies the others but not conversely, and sub-ranking and top-k calibration are incomparable. Empirically, we find popular label ranking models are often poorly calibrated, with substantial differences between sub-ranking and top-k metrics. Applying our framework to RLHF reward models, we find that calibration correlates strongly but not perfectly with benchmark accuracy, suggesting it captures a meaningful quality dimension beyond top-1 accuracy. These findings motivate future work on understanding the downstream effects of miscalibration and developing methods to correct it.

</details>


### [80] [Generalized Intention Modeling in Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31318)
*Mateusz Odrowaz-Sypniewski,Jasmine Bayrooti,Ajay Shankar,Amanda Prorok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种任务自适应的对手建模框架，通过学习多种意图表示的性能驱动混合，解决了现有方法假设意图信息具有普遍代表性的局限性，在多种任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对手建模方法使用预先选定的情节信息（如对手下一动作或未来环境状态）来编码意图，假设这些信息具有普遍代表性。然而，实证研究表明意图往往依赖于任务和环境，现有方法的假设并不成立。

Method: 提出任务自适应对手建模框架，学习多种意图表示的性能驱动混合；引入新的意图表示方法，通过最大化与自智能体未来回报的互信息，捕获与性能最直接相关的对手信息。

Result: 在多样化任务中，该方法持续匹配或超越最先进基线方法的性能，并提供了关于何时以及为何不同对手建模策略能够成功的深入见解。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了现有对手建模方法中意图表示的局限性，通过自适应学习多种意图表示的混合，实现了更好的性能和可解释性，为非合作竞争性多智能体强化学习中的决策制定提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Modeling an opponent's intent is critical for effective decision-making in non-cooperative, competitive, and general-sum multi-agent reinforcement learning. Existing opponent modeling methods encode intent using an embedding derived from episode information chosen a priori, such as the opponent's next action or a future environment state, and use this to guide the ego-agent's behavior. These approaches assume that the chosen information is universally representative of intent; however, we show empirically that this is not the case as intentions are often task- and environment-dependent. To address this, we introduce a task-adaptive opponent modeling framework that learns a performance-driven mixture of multiple intent representations. We further introduce a new intention representation that maximizes mutual information with the ego-agent's future returns, thereby capturing opponent information that is most directly relevant to performance. Our approach consistently matches or exceeds the performance of state-of-the-art baselines across diverse tasks and yields insights into when and why different opponent modeling strategies succeed.

</details>


### [81] [Bounded Behavioral Indistinguishability for Black-Box LLM Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30448)
*Munawar Hasan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出有界行为不可区分性作为黑盒LLM蒸馏的新评估框架，发现语义相似度提升不等于学生模型行为上与教师模型不可区分，揭示了现有评估方法的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的黑盒LLM蒸馏评估将输出匹配作为成功标准，认为学生模型的回答与教师模型语义相似或任务一致即可。然而，输出相似并不意味着学生在行为上与其模仿的模型无法区分，这种评估方法存在根本性缺陷。

Method: 提出有界行为不可区分性概念，形式化为(ε,q,t,𝔸)-行为不可区分性，其中ε界定区分优势、q界定oracle查询次数、t界定计算量、𝔸表示敌手类别。在Qwen和Llama师生对上使用5,000提示词的行为探测套件进行实验，比较教师与基础学生模型及LoRA蒸馏学生模型，并设计了成对教师识别敌手、A/B交换一致性过滤等对抗性评估方法。

Result: LoRA蒸馏将Qwen和Llama的语义相似度分别从0.788提升至0.862、从0.814提升至0.874。但对抗性评估显示行为差异仍然存在：学习判别器保持非零优势，成对类别分析显示伪影集中在风格/格式、鲁棒性和领域技术提示词类别。使用不同家族的Llama判断器和A/B交换一致性过滤后，Qwen区分优势从基础学生的0.158降至LoRA蒸馏后的0.081。查询预算实验表明，分歧引导获取并不一致优于分层随机采样，覆盖度和多样性仍是强基线。

Conclusion: 语义保真度有用但不足：黑盒LLM蒸馏需要有界、对抗性和类别感知的评估框架。仅依靠语义相似度无法确保学生模型在行为上与教师模型真正不可区分，需要更全面的评估方法。

Abstract: Black-box LLM distillation is usually evaluated as an output-matching problem: a student is considered successful when its responses are semantically similar to, or task-consistent with, those of a teacher. However, output similarity does not imply that the student is behaviorally indistinguishable from the model it imitates. We introduce bounded behavioral indistinguishability, formalized as $(ε,q,t,\mathbb{A})$-behavioral indistinguishability over an explicit prompt distribution, where $ε$ bounds distinguishing advantage, $q$ bounds oracle queries, $t$ bounds computation, and $\mathbb{A}$ denotes the adversary class.
  We instantiate this notion on Qwen and Llama teacher-student pairs using a controlled $5,000$-prompt behavioral probe suite.
