We generalize that foundation by introducing an orthogonality principle between the output score (the gradient of loss with respect to the final-layer output) and hidden-layer activations, which holds whenever the optimal score has conditional mean zero. This single principle unifies broadcast-based credit assignment across the standard differentiable-loss families, including cross-entropy, Bregman divergences, proper scoring rules, and exponential-family negative log-likelihoods. The framework supplies a theoretical grounding for the three-factor learning rule under general losses, with the neuromodulatory factor derived as the broadcast loss score. We derive the cross-entropy case explicitly, characterize the admissible loss class, and introduce a score vector expansion technique that enriches the broadcast signal while preserving the orthogonality framework. Experiments on CIFAR-10 and Tiny ImageNet show that SBD substantially improves over existing broadcast approaches, with score vector expansion delivering further gains. Overall, this work identifies the loss score as the signal to broadcast, supplies the orthogonality theory and theoretical grounding for the three-factor learning rule from neuroscience, and shows how score vector expansion enriches the decorrelation directions of the resulting objective.

</details>


### [117] [CSULoRA: Closest Safe Update Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30640)
*Oleksandr Marchenko Breneur,Adelaide Danilov,Aria Nourbakhsh,Salima Lamsiyah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CSULoRA，一种后处理方法，通过估计安全对齐子空间并对LoRA更新进行分量分解，以平滑衰减潜在不安全方向的方式校正已训练的LoRA适配器，在对抗性微调实验中显著降低攻击成功率并保留大部分效用增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低秩适应(LoRA)已成为大语言模型参数高效微调的标准方法，但少量不安全或对抗性微调数据即可显著削弱已对齐模型的安全行为。现有安全保护LoRA方法多依赖投影、剪枝、阈值化或额外训练目标等硬干预手段，这些方法虽能抑制不安全更新方向，但也可能移除任务相关信息或需要额外调参。

Method: CSULoRA是一种后处理方法，首先从安全对齐模型与对应基础检查点之间的权重位移中估计安全对齐子空间，然后将每个LoRA更新分解为完全对齐、部分对齐和离子空间三个分量。该方法不直接丢弃安全子空间外的分量，而是求解一个闭式惩罚最小变化问题，在保留完全对齐分量的同时，根据相对能量平滑衰减潜在不安全方向。

Result: 在对抗性微调实验中，CSULoRA显著降低了攻击成功率，同时保留了标准LoRA微调所获得的大部分效用增益。

Conclusion: CSULoRA通过最近安全更新估计成功校正了已训练的LoRA适配器，在不牺牲任务效用的前提下有效维护了模型安全性，为参数高效微调中的安全保护提供了一种更优雅的解决方案。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation has become a standard method for parameter-efficient fine-tuning of large language models, but even small amounts of unsafe or adversarial fine-tuning data can substantially weaken the safety behavior of aligned models. Existing safety-preserving LoRA methods often rely on hard interventions such as projection, pruning, thresholding, or additional training objectives. While these methods can suppress unsafe update directions, they may also remove task-relevant information or require extra tuning. We introduce CSULoRA, a post-hoc method for correcting trained LoRA adapters through closest safe update estimation. CSULoRA estimates a safety-aligned subspace from the weight displacement between a safety-aligned model and its corresponding base checkpoint. It then decomposes each LoRA update into fully aligned, partially aligned, and off-subspace components. Instead of discarding components outside the estimated safety subspace, CSULoRA solves a closed-form penalized minimum-change problem that preserves the fully aligned component while smoothly attenuating potentially unsafe directions according to their relative energy. In adversarial fine-tuning experiments, CSULoRA substantially reduces attack success rate while preserving most of the utility gains obtained from standard LoRA fine-tuning.

</details>


### [118] [Diffusion Models Preferentially Memorize Prototypical Examples or: Why Does My Diffusion Model Love Slop?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30642)
*Marta Aparicio Rodriguez,Anastasia Borovykh,Grigorios A. Pavliotis,Daniel J. Korchinski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现生成模型并非优先记忆罕见样本，而是优先记忆由常见子串组成的样本；数据集多样性（尤其是高层次抽象层面）是延缓记忆化的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型存在记忆训练数据的问题，可能导致法律风险并削弱创造性多样性。理解哪些样本会被记忆以及在何种条件下被记忆是一个重要的开放性问题，本文旨在回答"非典型或罕见样本是否会被优先记忆"这一问题。

Method: 在随机层次模型（RHM）生成的字符串上训练扩散模型，通过控制子串的出现频率和数据分布特性，系统研究样本的记忆化规律。

Result: 研究发现：1）由常见子串组成的样本被优先记忆，即使训练数据完全唯一也是如此，表明数据点级别的去重无法提供有意义的隐私保证；2）厚尾数据集（包含更多非典型样本）的记忆化被延迟，当高层次生产规则引入厚尾分布时该效应被放大；3）存在部分记忆化的中间阶段，常见子串被优先学习并在生成过程中被过度产出，若在此阶段停止训练会导致输出趋向平庸。

Conclusion: 数据集多样性（特别是高层次抽象层面的多样性）在延缓记忆化方面发挥重要作用。研究揭示了生成模型中"平庸化"现象的机制，为构建更具创造性和隐私安全的生成模型提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Generative models have a persistent limitation: their tendency to memorize training data can create legal liabilities and erode creative diversity. Understanding which samples are memorized in whole or in part, and under what conditions, therefore remains an important open problem. Here we answer the question "Are atypical or rare samples memorized first?" in the negative. We train diffusion models on strings generated according to the production rules of the Random Hierarchy Model (RHM), and find that samples composed of common substrings are preferentially memorized. This holds true even if the training data consists of entirely unique samples, indicating that deduplication at the data point level does not provide a meaningful privacy guarantee. Correspondingly we predict, then observe, delayed memorization for fat-tailed datasets (i.e., those with more atypical samples). This effect is amplified when fat-tails are introduced into high-level production rules. These together suggest that dataset diversity, particularly at higher levels of abstraction, plays an important role in staving off memorization. Finally, we identify an intermediate regime of partial memorization in which common substrings are learned first and subsequently overproduced during generation. If training is stopped in this regime, models will exhibit the reversion-to-the-mean blandness often derided as "slop".

</details>


### [119] [Convergence of Steepest Descent and Adam under Non-Uniform Smoothness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30648)
*Sharan Vaswani,Yifan Sun,Reza Babanezhad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将非均匀平滑假设推广至曲率与目标函数值成仿射关系的情形，证明在此条件下（配合梯度主导条件），最速下降法、RMSProp和Adam的对角变体均能收敛，并且明确证明了RMSProp和Adam在特定场景下比GD、AdaGrad、AMSGrad和heavy-ball momentum更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作分析了非均匀平滑假设下一阶方法的收敛性，但这些假设可能无法充分刻画机器学习任务中损失景观的全部特性。本文旨在将这一假设推广到更广泛的情况——目标函数的曲率是目标函数值的仿射函数，从而涵盖逻辑回归、广义线性模型、softmax策略梯度以及特定神经网络等更广泛的问题类别。

Method: 作者提出了曲率与目标函数值成仿射关系的新假设，并在该假设下结合梯度主导条件，分析了最速下降法以及确定性对角版本的RMSProp和Adam算法的收敛性，建立了通用的收敛速率结果。此外，还通过构造下界来比较不同算法的性能。

Result: 1. 在逻辑回归（可分数据）和softmax策略梯度目标上，sign GD（符号梯度下降）以线性速率收敛，且理论上比标准GD更快。
2. 对于特定两类神经网络在可分数据上的训练，RMSProp和Adam使用常数步长和动量参数可以线性收敛。
3. 证明了下界，表明在该假设下RMSProp和Adam理论上比AdaGrad、AMSGrad、梯度下降和heavy-ball momentum更快。

Conclusion: 本文成功将非均匀平滑条件推广至曲率为目标函数值仿射函数的情形，为一大类机器学习问题（包括逻辑回归、广义线性模型、强化学习中的策略梯度方法以及神经网络）提供了更紧密的理论分析。研究结果揭示了符号梯度方法、RMSProp和Adam在特定条件下的优越性，为理解和设计优化算法提供了新的理论依据。

Abstract: Recent work has analyzed the convergence of first-order methods under non-uniform smoothness assumptions that better model the loss landscape in machine learning tasks. We generalize this assumption to objectives whose curvature is an affine function of the objective value. This property is satisfied by a broad class of problems, including logistic regression, generalized linear models with a logistic link function, softmax policy gradient in reinforcement learning, and a class of neural networks. Under this assumption and gradient domination conditions, we establish a general convergence rate for the steepest descent method, and deterministic, diagonal variants of RMSProp and Adam. Our results imply that for logistic regression on separable data and the softmax policy gradient objective, sign GD converges linearly and is provably faster than GD. Furthermore, we show that for a class of two-layer neural networks on separable data, RMSProp and Adam can converge at a linear rate with a constant step-size and momentum parameter. Finally, we present a lower bound demonstrating that, under our assumption, RMSProp and Adam are provably faster than AdaGrad, AMSGrad, gradient descent, and heavy-ball momentum.

</details>


### [120] [LARK: Learnability-Grounded Trajectory Selection for Efficient Reasoning Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30651)
*Tianrun Yu,Kaixiang Zhao,Chih-Chun Chen,Amanda Hughes,Taylor W. Killian,Fenglong Ma,Weitong Zhang,Porter Jenkins*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了LARK方法，用于推理蒸馏中的轨迹选择，核心思想是选择学生模型能够高效学习的轨迹，而非仅依赖轨迹质量或模型置信度等启发式方法。通过引入可学习性因子和正则化选择策略，LARK在多个模型和任务上超越了现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推理轨迹选择方法主要依赖轨迹质量或模型置信度等启发式规则，但往往忽略了轨迹是否对学生模型具有可学习性。这可能导致选择的轨迹虽然质量高，但学生模型难以有效学习。因此需要一种能够评估和选择对学生模型而言高效可学习轨迹的方法。

Method: 提出LARK方法，核心是可学习性因子ρ，用于刻画学生模型训练损失下降的速率。为了高效估计该速率并保持泛化性，引入了可学习性代理和χ²正则化选择策略，前者用于高效估计学习速率，后者用于平衡可学习性和分布覆盖，两者都具有理论保证的估计误差界限。

Result: LARK在多个基础模型和推理任务上始终优于数据选择基线方法。诊断分析表明LARK分数能够预测下游训练效果，且LARK选择的轨迹能够诱导更快的监督微调损失下降。

Conclusion: 通过将可学习性作为轨迹选择的核心准则，LARK成功解决了现有方法忽视学生学习能力的问题。该方法不仅在实验中取得了优越性能，还提供了理论保证，为推理蒸馏中的数据选择提供了新的视角和有效解决方案。

Abstract: We study trajectory selection for reasoning distillation, where teacher-generated reasoning trajectories are selectively used as supervision for a student model. Existing methods rely on heuristics such as trajectory quality or model confidence, but they often overlook whether a trajectory is learnable by the student. In this paper, we present LARK, a learnability-grounded method for reasoning trajectory selection. LARK selects trajectories that the student can learn efficiently while preserving the generalization of the full training distribution. At the core of LARK is a learnability factor $ρ$, which characterizes the rate at which the student's training loss decreases. To estimate this rate efficiently and maintain generalization, we introduce a learnability proxy and a $χ^2$-regularized selection policy that balances learnability and distributional coverage, both with strong theoretical guarantees on their estimation error. Empirically, LARK consistently outperforms data selection baselines across multiple base models and reasoning tasks. Diagnostic analyses show that the LARK score predicts downstream training utility and that LARK-selected trajectories induce faster supervised fine-tuning loss reduction. Our code is available at https://github.com/Tianrun-Yu/LARK.

</details>


### [121] [Bridging the Gap Between Natural Language and Market Dynamics via High-Dimensional Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30652)
*Yujin Jeong,Noelle Jung,Brian Y. C. Leung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出用高维FinBERT嵌入替代传统标量情感分数，通过Siamese网络优化嵌入表示，提升了短期股价预测的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的多模态金融预测依赖标量情感分数，无法捕捉金融新闻的细微差别，导致信息丢失。

Method: 在基于Transformer的预测架构中，用密集的FinBERT嵌入替代离散极性评分；在FNSPID数据集上对比了原始嵌入、注意力加权聚合和自定义Siamese网络三种嵌入策略。

Result: 基于注意力的机制受金融数据低信噪比影响表现不佳；集成Siamese优化嵌入的方法优于标量基线和原始嵌入方法。

Conclusion: 保留高维叙事上下文能够提升短期股价变动的预测准确性。

Abstract: Traditional multi-modal financial forecasting often relies on scalar sentiment scores, which fail to capture the nuances of financial news. To address this information loss, this paper explores high-dimensional representation learning by replacing discrete polarity ratings with dense FinBERT embeddings within a Transformer-based forecasting architecture. We benchmarked various embedding strategies on the FNSPID dataset, including raw embeddings, attention-weighted aggregation, and a custom Siamese network. While the attention-based mechanism struggled with the low signal-to-noise ratio typical of financial data, the integration of Siamese-optimized embeddings outperformed both the scalar baseline and raw embedding approaches, demonstrating that preserving high-dimensional narrative context yields improved predictive accuracy for short-term stock price movements.

</details>


### [122] [Learning to Perceive the World Through Control: Empowerment-Based Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30656)
*Mahsa Bastankhah,Sophie Broderick,Benjamin Eysenbach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究强化学习中的表征学习问题，发现通过最大化 empowerment 目标，智能体能够学习到前向和后向两种互补的、对控制无关特征具有不变性的表征，从而获得以控制为中心的世界模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际强化学习环境中，观测维度往往远高于控制相关变量的维度。作者提出核心问题：能否学习仅捕获控制相关特征的表征？这一问题具有重要的理论和实践意义，因为去除控制无关特征可以提高学习效率和泛化能力。

Method: 通过研究 empowerment 目标来解决该问题。Empowerment 最大化智能体对环境的影响能力，广泛应用于无监督技能学习。作者分析发现，empowerment 智能体会诱导产生两种不同的表征——前向表征和后向表征，它们分别捕获状态的互补方面，且都对控制无关特征具有不变性。

Result: 研究结果表明，empowerment 最大化使智能体学习到隐式的、以控制为中心的世界模型。这种通过交互学习表征的方式能够获得有用的不变性性质，而仅从被动数据集中学习则无法达到相同效果。

Conclusion: 该工作强调了通过交互而非被动数据集学习表征的重要性：以最大化控制为目标的交互对于学习有用的不变性性质至关重要，这一观点与因果学习文献中的发现高度一致，为强化学习中的表征学习提供了新的理论视角和实践指导。

Abstract: In many practical reinforcement learning environments, observations are far higher-dimensional than the variables that matter for control. In this work, we ask: can we learn representations that capture only control-relevant features of the environment? We study this question through the empowerment objective, which maximizes an agent's influence over the environment and is widely used for unsupervised skill learning. We show that empowerment agents induce two distinct representations -- forward and backward -- that capture complementary aspects of the state, and both of which are invariant to control-irrelevant features. Thus, empowerment maximization leads agents to learn an implicit, control-centric model of the world. Our analysis highlights the importance of learning representations through interaction rather than from passive datasets: interaction aimed at maximizing control is essential for learning useful invariance properties, a perspective that aligns closely with the causal learning literature.

</details>


### [123] [BOKBO (Best of K Bad Options): Calibrated Abstention for VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30660)
*Anya Singh,Cabrel Happi,Jai Relan,Varun Nair,Vidyut Baradwaj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了BOKBO，首个面向K样本视觉-语言-动作（VLA）推断的共形弃权层，提供有限样本无分布保证的执行违规率控制，并揭示了现有方法中非共形分数的结构性失效问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的测试时扩展VLA策略方法（如RoboMonkey、SEAL等）在推理时采样K个候选动作并执行验证器选出的最佳动作。然而，当所有K个候选动作都不安全时，系统会在无警告的情况下执行违规动作，缺乏安全保证机制。

Method: 提出BOKBO共形弃权层，包含全局和任务特定（Mondrian）两种变体；分析揭示基于扰动的K采样中策略内部非共形分数存在结构性失效——基策略置信度代理与K样本分歧与噪声超参数σ高度相关（0.98），但与实际安全违规的相关性仅为噪声基底水平；引入基于语义视觉特征和任务身份的违规预测器实现紧密校准。

Result: 在OpenVLA-OFT上，ε=0.05设置下，86%的Bootstrap分割满足条件CRC边界，覆盖率达78%，任务成功率70%；Mondrian-BOKBO将最小任务条件保持分数从0.71提升至0.93；结果在5个训练种子上稳定，在π₀-FAST上可复现，在四个分布偏移下保持有效。

Conclusion: BOKBO成功解决了K样本VLA推断的安全保证问题，Mondrian变体缩小了困难任务上的条件差距；同时识别并修正了一个方法论缺陷：全局设定的力阈值远低于专家典型操作力，导致将正常操作误判为不安全行为，违规率被夸大5倍。

Abstract: Test-time scaling for vision-language-action (VLA) policies, methods such as RoboMonkey, SEAL, MG-Select, and V-GPS, samples K candidate action chunks at inference and executes the verifier-best. When all K candidates are unsafe, the system executes a violating action with no warning. We propose BOKBO, the first conformal abstention layer for K-sample VLA inference, providing finite-sample distribution-free guarantees on executed-violation rate. We provide both global and per-task (Mondrian) variants, with the per-task variant closing the conditional gap on the hardest tasks.
  Our analysis exposes a structural failure of policy-internal nonconformity scores under perturbation-based K-sampling: the base-policy confidence proxy and K-sample disagreement correlate at 0.98 with the action-noise hyperparameter $σ$, while correlating at the noise floor with actual safety violations. We test the failure's scope by replicating the analysis under token-level temperature sampling and find the failure is mechanism-specific and partially mitigated under policy-stochasticity-based sampling. A learned violation predictor conditioned on semantic visual features and task identity supports tight calibration: at $ε$ = 0.05 on libero_object_temp_x0.1 with OpenVLA-OFT, the conditional CRC bound holds on 86% of bootstrap splits with 78% coverage and 70% net task success. Mondrian-BOKBO raises the minimum per-task conditional hold fraction from 0.71 to 0.93. Results are stable across 5 training seeds, replicate within bootstrap noise on $π_0$-FAST, hold on libero_spatial_temp_x0.1 as a co-equal benchmark, and survive four within-suite distribution shifts. We additionally identify and correct a methodological pitfall: globally-set force thresholds well below expert-typical manipulation forces conflate unsafe behavior with normal manipulation, inflating violation rates by $5\times$.

</details>


### [124] [Spatio-temporal stochastic graph-based learning for infectious disease forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30662)
*Luz Stefani Sotomayor Valenzuela,Susanna Cramb,Darren Wraith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种时空随机图架构，用于预测传染病新发病例。该模型能够统一处理不同人口规模的地理网络，在预测美国COVID-19和匈牙利水痘数据时表现出具有竞争力的整体性能，能够反映疫情总体进展趋势，但存在一步延迟且对高频低幅变化敏感度较低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时空图模型通常用于预测COVID-19和水痘等传染病的新发病例，但随机建模在其学习过程中的应用被严重忽视，且很少考虑大型国家的完整数据集。因此，这些模型在真实世界疾病传播场景中是否能提供准确预测尚不清楚。

Method: 提出了一种时空随机图架构，整合了随机公式化和不确定性近似过程来预测传染病新发病例。该架构能够适应单一模型架构中大规模和小规模人口的地理网络编码。使用美国COVID-19和匈牙利水痘两个真实世界数据集进行验证，并与四种时空图模型进行基准对比。

Result: 在美国COVID-19 2022年第一波预测中展现出增强效果，在匈牙利2012-2014年水痘波次的比较中也表现良好。定量结果显示，在预测美国3,218个县和匈牙利20个县的所有新病例时，该方法具有具有竞争力的每周整体性能。该方法能够反映相对于基准方法的疫情总体进展，但存在一步延迟，同时表现出对高频低幅变异性的敏感性降低。

Conclusion: 该研究所提出的时空随机图架构成功将随机建模整合到疾病预测过程中，为大规模国家的传染病预测提供了一种有效的新方法。模型在保持预测准确性的同时，对数据中的高频噪声具有更强的鲁棒性，为公共卫生决策提供了可靠的预测工具。

Abstract: Spatio-temporal graph-based models have typically been used to forecast new cases of infectious diseases such as COVID-19 and chickenpox outbreaks. However, the use of stochastic modelling into their learning process has been surprisingly under-investigated and rarely considered entire data sets of large countries. As a result, it is unknown whether these models would provide accurate forecasts in real-world disease spread scenarios. In this work, we propose a spatio-temporal stochastic graph-based architecture that integrates a stochastic formulation and uncertainty approximation process to forecast new infectious disease cases. We find that our approach can adapt to encode large and small population geographical networks within a single model architecture. Using two real-world data sets, COVID-19 in the US and chickenpox in Hungary, we report an enhanced effect of the proposed architecture across predictions of the 2022 first wave for COVID-19 in the US and comparative results of chickenpox waves during 2012-2014 in Hungary. By benchmarking with four spatio-temporal graph-based models, quantitative results show competitive overall weekly performance of the proposed approach on forecasting new cases for all 3,218 US counties and all 20 Hungary counties. The proposed approach can represent overall epidemic progression relative to baselines, though with a one-step delay; while exhibiting a reduced sensitivity to high-frequency and low-amplitude variability.

</details>


### [125] [Universal Decision Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30694)
*Sridhar Mahadevan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了通用决策学习器（UDL），一种用范畴论统一多种决策理论（规划、强化学习、因果干预、在线学习、博弈均衡）的框架，通过左/右Kan延拓分别表达策略展开与一致性约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有决策理论（规划、强化学习、因果干预、在线学习、博弈均衡）各自独立发展，缺乏统一视角；本文旨在提供一个通用的范畴论表述，以揭示这些理论背后共同的数学结构。

Method: 引入部分定义的决策函子，利用左Kan延拓建模策略展开、聚合与候选生成，利用右Kan延拓建模一致性、约束满足与不动点语义，从而将局部行为数据扩展到全局连贯行为。

Result: 给出了抽象的UDL构造，证明了其泛比较性质，定义了Kan不变行为等价性与最小抽象，并展示贝尔曼方程、规划递归、因果干预、在线遗憾与均衡均可作为特例出现。

Conclusion: 许多决策形式主义实例化同一个泛问题：先以典范方式扩展局部行为数据，再刻画全局连贯扩展；这为理解不同决策算法之间的关系提供了统一的数学视角。

Abstract: Many theories of decision making -- planning, reinforcement learning, causal intervention, online learning, and game-theoretic equilibrium -- turn local information into globally coherent behavior. This paper proposes a common categorical formulation: a Universal Decision Learner (UDL) extends a partially specified decision functor from observed contexts to new contexts by a pair of universal constructions. Left Kan extensions express rollout, aggregation, and candidate generation; right Kan extensions express consistency, constraint satisfaction, and fixed-point semantics. The central claim is not that every decision problem has the same algorithm, but that many decision formalisms instantiate the same universal problem: extend local behavioral data canonically, then characterize the globally coherent extensions. We give the abstract UDL construction, prove its universal comparison property, define Kan-invariant behavioral equivalence and minimal abstractions, and show how Bellman equations, planning recursions, causal interventions, online regret, and equilibria arise as special cases. The supplementary material develops the reinforcement-learning specialization in more detail.

</details>


### [126] [A Context-Aware Middleware for Medical Image Based Reports: An approach based on image feature extraction and association rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30699)
*Erick O. Rodrigues,Jose Viterbo,Aura Conci,Trueman Mac Henry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种上下文感知中间件，通过收集分析医务人员处理医学图像的数据并结合临床上下文，自动推断并分派新到的医学图像给最适合的医务人员，以提高医疗工作流效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在医院、实验室和远程放射科中，医务人员各有专业专长，医学图像需转发给特定医生或团队。现有的转发过程需要反复判断最佳人选及其当前可用性，既耗时又低效，成为医疗工作流中的瓶颈。

Method: 开发了一种上下文感知中间件，能够收集并处理每位医务人员分析医学图像时产生的数据，结合当前临床上下文信息，通过推理机制确定最适合处理新到达医学图像的医务人员。

Result: 该中间件能够基于历史处理数据和当前上下文，自动推断并选择最适合的医务人员接收特定类型的医学图像，减少了人工判断和转发的时间消耗。

Conclusion: 所提出的中间件有效解决了医学图像转发过程中的低效问题，通过上下文感知和智能推理实现了工作流的自动化优化，可显著提升医疗机构的工作效率和诊断流程质量。

Abstract: This work proposes a context-aware middleware for medical workflow organization and efficiency improvement. In hospitals, laboratories and teleradiology companies, each physician or technician is specialized in a specific kind of diagnosis or analysis. Therefore, certain types of medical images are often forwarded to a certain physician or a certain group. This forwarding is time consuming. That is, repeatedly deciding who would be the best physician, whether he is available at a certain moment given a certain context is exhaustive and may be very inefficient. Thus, the proposed middleware has the ability to process and collect data from images analyzed by each medical staff. Based on the collected data and current clinical context, the middleware is able to infer who would be the best fit staff to receive a certain incoming medical image.

</details>


### [127] [Diversity Matters: Revisiting Test-Time Compute in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30713)
*Yijie Tong,Yifan Hou,Shaobo Cui,Antoine Bosselut,Mrinmaya Sachan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了基于熵的测试时计算策略（ETTC），通过利用预测熵选择最置信的预测，在多模型集成中有效解决了标准多数投票因缺乏预测多样性而收益有限的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时计算（TTC）策略已被证明能提升大语言模型的推理能力，但其在视觉语言模型（VLM）中的应用和效益仍未被充分探索。现有方法（如基于特征的评分和多数投票）在单模型设置中收益有限，缺乏预测多样性。

Method: 提出了基于熵的测试时计算策略（ETTC），基于预测熵选择最置信的预测。该方法在单模型情况下退化为多数投票，而在多模型集成中，利用置信度差异优先考虑更强的模型。

Result: 在七个VLM和六个基准上进行了系统研究，证明了ETTC在温和假设下优于多数投票。实验表明，ETTC始终优于多数投票和最佳单个模型，且较小的模型可以协同增强较大的模型。

Conclusion: ETTC为视觉语言模型提供了一种有效的测试时计算策略，通过模型集成实现了标准策略无法达到的性能提升，展示了小模型与大模型协同增效的潜力。

Abstract: Test-time compute (TTC) strategies have emerged as a lightweight approach to boost reasoning in large language models (LLMs). However, their application and benefits for vision-language models (VLMs) remain underexplored. We present a systematic study of TTC across seven VLMs and six benchmarks, specifically analyzing feature-based scoring and majority voting methods. We find that feature heuristics fail and voting yields only modest gains in single-model settings. We theoretically show that this limitation stems from a lack of prediction diversity: when outputs are highly correlated, voting provides little benefit. In contrast, multi-model ensembles offer richer diversity, yet standard majority voting fails to account for varying model capabilities. To address this, we propose Entropy-based TTC (ETTC), which selects the most confident prediction based on predictive entropy. Our method reduces to majority voting in the single-model case, but in model ensembles, it leverages confidence disparities to prioritize stronger models. We prove that ETTC outperforms majority voting under mild assumptions and empirically demonstrate that it consistently surpasses both voting and the best individual model. Crucially, our results show that smaller models can synergistically enhance larger ones, unlocking ensembling gains not achievable with standard strategies.

</details>


### [128] [When are LLMs Sufficient Policy Optimizers for Sequential RL Tasks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30719)
*Stephane Hatgis-Kessell,Emma Brunskill*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出PromptPO方法，证明LLM可作为强化学习的黑盒策略优化器，在复杂探索任务中表现优异，但在需要精细连续控制的场景中存在局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究大语言模型（LLM）何时能够替代传统强化学习算法，作为有效的黑盒策略优化器。核心问题是在什么条件下可以用LLM替代经典RL算法进行策略优化。

Method: 提出Prompted Policy Optimization (PromptPO)迭代方法：通过向LLM提供状态空间、动作空间和奖励函数的Python描述，让LLM生成可执行策略，并根据rollout反馈进行迭代优化，无需显式编程即可自动生成从比例控制器到规划算法的多样化策略。

Result: 在困难探索环境、Meta-World机器人任务和多个实际控制问题中，PromptPO的性能通常达到或超过标准RL基线，同时使用更少的环境交互次数；但在MuJoCo连续控制域中表现不如标准RL基线，揭示了LLM策略优化在精细连续控制场景中的局限性。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的策略优化在LLM能够利用环境先验知识或优化策略知识时是充分的，但在需要精细连续控制的场景中存在明显局限性，这为LLM在强化学习中的应用边界提供了重要见解。

Abstract: We study when large language models (LLMs) can serve as effective black-box policy optimizers for reinforcement learning (RL) tasks, i.e., when can we replace classical RL algorithms with an LLM? We explore this question by introducing Prompted Policy Optimization (PromptPO), an iterative method that prompts an LLM with Python descriptions of the state space, action space, and reward function, then has it generate and refine executable policies based on rollout feedback. Across hard exploration environments, Meta-World robotics tasks, and several real-world control problems, PromptPO often matches or exceeds the performance of standard RL baselines while using substantially fewer environment interactions. To maximize expected return, and without further explicit prompting, the policies PromptPO outputs range from tuned proportional controllers or rule-based plans to policies that run planning algorithms like value iteration. Our results demonstrate that LLM-based policy optimization is sufficient when the LLM can leverage prior knowledge about the environment or optimization strategy. PromptPO underperforms standard RL baselines in MuJoCo domains. This demonstrates possible limitations of LLM-based policy optimization to settings that requiring fine-grained continuous control.

</details>


### [129] [Kalimati Vegetable Price Index Forecasting with a Momentum Corrected Online Stacking Ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30720)
*Sahaj Raj Malla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了KVPI蔬菜价格指数，并通过动量校正在线堆叠集成模型在新兴经济体农产品价格预测上取得了优异性能（90天预测MAPE仅0.68%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴经济体的农产品价格预测因高波动性、频繁供应中断和文化因素影响而极具挑战性，现有研究缺乏针对此类市场的有效预测工具。

Method: 构建了基于135种蔬菜、跨10年数据的KVPI逆向波动率加权复合指数，开发了64个因果有效特征（包括节日滞后效应、滚动统计量等），系统评估了14种预测模型（统计、树模型、深度学习、混合和Transformer架构）在多时间尺度上的表现。

Result: 树模型集成表现最为稳健，统计模型和复杂Transformer在噪声数据上表现不佳；所提动量校正在线堆叠集成模型在90天预测中达到RMSE=1.771、MAPE=0.68%、R²=0.845的最优性能。

Conclusion: 该开源预测管道为尼泊尔及类似市场的政策制定者和供应链参与者提供了实用可靠的价格预测工具，有助于增强粮食安全保障能力。

Abstract: Forecasting agricultural commodity prices in emerging economies is difficult due to high volatility, frequent supply disruptions, and strong cultural influences on demand. This study introduces the Kalimati Vegetable Price Index (KVPI), a new inverse-volatility weighted composite index that aggregates 135 daily wholesale commodities from Kathmandu over ten years (2013-2023). By creating a stable macro-level signal, the KVPI reduces the noise inherent in modelling individual crops. A rich set of 64 causally valid features was developed, including festival lead-lag effects, rolling statistics, and calendar variables. Fourteen forecasting models spanning statistical, tree-based, deep learning, hybrid, and transformer architectures were rigorously evaluated across short (7-day), medium (14- and 30-day), and long-term (90-day) horizons. Tree-based ensembles proved notably robust, while classical statistical models and complex transformers struggled with the noisy dataset. The proposed Momentum-Corrected Online Stacking Ensemble achieved the strongest performance, yielding a Root Mean Square Error (RMSE) of 1.771, an exceptionally low Mean Absolute Percentage Error (MAPE) of 0.68%, and explaining 84.5% of the variance (R-squared = 0.845) at the 90-day horizon. This open-source pipeline provides policymakers and supply chain actors in Nepal and similar markets with a practical, reliable tool for anticipating price movements and strengthening food security.

</details>


### [130] [Self-Certifying Transport MCMC via Dual Spectral-Gap Certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30722)
*Jun Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了CerT-MCMC框架，首次为基于学习传输的MCMC方法提供了自动、严格的收敛性证明。通过归一化流构建两种互补的收敛证明：覆盖证明(covering certificate)和分位数核证明(quantile-core certificate)，后者在高维情况下表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于学习传输的MCMC方法（如使用归一化流的Metropolis-Hastings）缺乏自动化的收敛性证明机制。虽然这些方法在实践中表现良好，但无法区分真实的传输失败与证明技术的局限性，缺乏理论保证。因此需要开发能够自动提供严格收敛保证的框架。

Method: 使用归一化流将高斯参考分布映射到目标后验的近似，该流同时作为独立Metropolis-Hastings提议分布和可计算谱间隙界的基础。开发了两种互补的收敛证明：(1)覆盖证明通过有限样本覆盖论证约束权重比的振荡，产生全支撑谱间隙界；(2)分位数核证明将注意力限制在高概率残差核上，通过一维经验分位数控制振荡。

Result: 覆盖证明的修正项按O(n^{-1/D})缩放，在维数增加时快速减弱直至无效；证明了匹配的Ω(n^{-1/D})下界，确认这是点式Lipschitz证明固有的障碍。分位数核证明的有限样本概率松弛为O(n^{-1/2})，独立于环境维数。在合成目标(D=2-20)、结构工程后验(D=6,8)、Heart Disease数据集的逻辑回归(D=13)和合成贝叶斯逻辑回归(D=20)上，分位数核证明在覆盖证明无效时提供了有效的谱间隙界，其谱间隙代理与经验有效样本量偏差在7%以内。负面对照实验证实证明能以超过10倍因子区分流质量，而接受率仅相差1.15倍。

Conclusion: 双证明框架是首个为学习传输MCMC提供自动、维数感知收敛证明的方法，能够区分真实的传输失败与证明技术限制。分位数核证明在高维情况下优于覆盖证明，为实际应用提供了可操作的理论保证。

Abstract: We propose CerT-MCMC, a framework that equips learned-transport Markov chain Monte Carlo with automatic, rigorous convergence certificates. A normalising flow maps a Gaussian reference to an approximation of the target posterior; the same flow then serves as both the independence Metropolis-Hastings proposal and the basis for a computable spectral-gap bound. We develop two complementary certificates. The covering certificate bounds the weight-ratio oscillation over the full proposal support via finite-sample covering arguments, yielding full-support spectral-gap bounds when a conservative gradient bound is available; its correction term scales as O(n^{-1/D}), making it rapidly weak and eventually vacuous as dimension increases. We prove a matching Omega(n^{-1/D}) lower bound, establishing that this barrier is intrinsic to pointwise Lipschitz certification. The quantile-core certificate restricts attention to a high-probability residual core on which the oscillation is controlled by one-dimensional empirical quantiles, with a finite-sample probability slack of O(n^{-1/2}), independent of the ambient dimension. On synthetic targets (D=2-20), structural-engineering posteriors (D=6,8), real-data logistic regression on the Heart Disease data set (D=13), and synthetic Bayesian logistic regression (D=20), the quantile-core certificate delivers non-vacuous spectral-gap bounds where the covering certificate is vacuous, and its spectral-gap proxy tracks empirical effective sample sizes within 7%. A negative control experiment confirms that the certificate discriminates flow quality by a factor exceeding 10x, whereas acceptance rates differ by only 1.15x. To our knowledge, the dual-certificate framework is the first to provide automatic, dimension-aware convergence certificates for learned-transport MCMC, distinguishing genuine transport failure from proof-technique limitations.

</details>


### [131] [OrcaRouter: A Production-Oriented LLM Router with Hybrid Offline-Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30736)
*Zhenghua Bao,Fengya Tian,Chris Zhang,Zhenjun Chen,Xile Ma,Yi Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OrcaRouter，一个面向生产环境的LLM路由器，结合LinUCB上下文老虎机和混合离线-在线学习协议，在RouterArena排行榜上排名第二，达到75.54%准确率，成本为每1000次查询1美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型的快速发展，不同模型具有不同的能力和推理成本，这带来了一个实际部署问题：对于传入的请求，应该由哪个模型来处理？需要一种智能路由策略来平衡性能和成本。

Method: OrcaRouter采用基于LinUCB的上下文老虎机算法，结合词汇和句子嵌入特征。离线阶段，在路由提示的精选集上评估每个候选模型以获得完全信息反馈，生成奖励矩阵并为每个臂拟合一个岭回归器。在线部署时，从这些参数初始化，并可选择性从老虎机反馈中继续学习，仅在观察到所选模型奖励后更新其对应的臂。

Result: 在RouterArena提交时（2026年5月20日），OrcaRouter-Adaptive在公共排行榜上排名第二，arena得分为72.08，在成本为每1000次查询1美元的情况下实现了75.54%的准确率。

Conclusion: OrcaRouter成功实现了面向生产环境的LLM路由，通过混合离线-在线学习协议有效平衡了模型性能与推理成本，在实际部署中取得了具有竞争力的结果。

Abstract: The rapid development of large language models, each with distinct capabilities and inference costs, raises a practical deployment question: given an incoming request, which model should handle it? We present OrcaRouter, a production-oriented LLM router that combines a LinUCB-based contextual bandit over lexical and sentence-embedding features with a hybrid offline-online learning protocol. Offline, OrcaRouter obtains full-information feedback by evaluating each candidate model on a curated set of routing prompts, yielding a reward matrix used to fit one ridge regressor per arm. At deployment time, it initializes from these parameters and can optionally continue learning from bandit feedback, updating only the selected model's arm after observing its reward. At the time of our RouterArena submission (May 20, 2026), OrcaRouter-Adaptive ranked second on the public RouterArena leaderboard with an arena score of 72.08, achieving 75.54% accuracy at a cost of USD 1.00 per 1,000 queries.

</details>


### [132] [SemStruct: Contextualizing Semantic Embeddings with Structural Information for Schema Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30729)
*Inwon Kang,Kavitha Srinivas,Nandana Mihindukulasooriya,Sola Shirai,Parikshit Ram,Horst Samulowitz,Oshani Seneviratne*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SemStruct框架，通过将冻结的预训练语言模型与图神经网络相结合，显式建模表格的结构信息（行级共现关系），在模式匹配任务上达到了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于预训练语言模型（PLM）的模式匹配方法通常将表格数据序列化为独立的列描述文本序列，这种序列化丢弃了关键的结构信息——特别是行级共现关系（即关系上下文），导致模型只能依赖列标题语义或独立分布进行匹配。这一局限性促使研究者寻求一种能够结合语义理解能力和结构信息的方法。

Method: 提出SemStruct框架，将表格建模为异构图，其中列和值作为节点，通过行进行连接。该方法将冻结的预训练语言模型与图神经网络（GNN）的结构归纳偏置相结合，允许GNN在图结构中传播消歧上下文。与其他需要专有大语言模型访问权限和语言模型微调的最先进方法不同，SemStruct保持语言模型冻结，仅训练轻量级的结构编码器。

Result: 在Valentine和SOTAB-SM基准数据集上进行了广泛实验，结果表明SemStruct达到了最先进的性能，在复杂的、语义可连接的数据集上优于完全微调的基线方法。消融研究还揭示了行表示主要作为拓扑传导媒介而非语义实体，验证了在模式匹配中显式结构建模的必要性。

Conclusion: SemStruct成功地将预训练语言模型的语义能力与图神经网络的结构归纳偏置相结合，通过显式建模表格的行级共现关系，有效解决了现有方法丢弃关键结构信息的问题。该方法在不依赖模型微调和专有LLM访问的情况下，实现了更优的模式匹配性能，证明了结构信息在模式匹配任务中的重要性。

Abstract: Schema matching is a fundamental step in integrating heterogeneous data sources. While Pre-trained Language Models (PLMs) have revolutionized this task by capturing linguistic semantics, they typically process tabular data as serialized text sequences of standalone column descriptions. This serialization discards critical structural information -- specifically, the row-level co-occurrences, i.e. the relational context -- forcing models to rely solely on column header semantics or standalone distributions. To bridge this gap, we propose SemStruct, a framework that joins the semantic power of frozen PLMs with the structural inductive bias of Graph Neural Networks (GNNs). We model the table as a heterogeneous graph where columns and values are nodes connected by rows, allowing the GNN to propagate disambiguating context across the structure. Unlike other state-of-the-art methods that require proprietary LLM access and fine-tuning of language models, SemStruct keeps the language model frozen and trains only a lightweight structural encoder. Extensive experiments on the Valentine and SOTAB-SM benchmarks demonstrate that SemStruct achieves state-of-the-art performance, outperforming fully fine-tuned baselines on complex, semantically joinable datasets. Furthermore, our ablation studies reveal that row representations serve primarily as topological conduits rather than semantic entities, validating the necessity of explicit structural modeling in schema matching.

</details>


### [133] [Smaller Models are Natural Explorers for Policy-Level Diversity in GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30789)
*Yiming Ren,Yiran Xu,Zicheng Lin,Chufan Shi,Yukang Chen,Dingdong Wang,Tianhe Wu,Junjie Wang,Yujiu Yang,Yu Qiao,Ruihang Chu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出S2L-PO框架，利用同族小模型作为探索器来训练大模型，相比token级噪声注入策略，在数学推理基准上提升8.8%准确率并降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GRPO方法通过注入token级随机性来增加rollout多样性，但这会引入逐步噪声并导致轨迹不连贯。作者发现同族小模型天然具有更高的策略级多样性（pass@k更高），这种多样性在时间上相关、保持逻辑一致性，可提供结构化的探索信号。

Method: 提出S2L-PO框架：利用固定的小模型作为自然探索器来训练大模型。设计了渐进式退火策略，从离线小模型rollout逐渐过渡到大模型自身的采样，以平衡探索与利用，避免因小模型容量限制导致的中期训练性能下降。

Result: 在多种数学推理基准上提升准确率，例如使用1.7B探索器指导8B模型在AIME 24上准确率提升8.8%，同时降低rollout计算成本，实现更快收敛和更高的性能上限。

Conclusion: 小模型作为探索器提供的策略级多样性优于token级噪声注入，能够在保持逻辑一致性的同时提升GRPO的训练效果，为大语言模型强化学习训练提供了一种更高效的探索范式。

Abstract: We identify a new dimension for enhancing rollout diversity in Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) for LLMs. While GRPO relies on diverse rollouts, prevailing strategies primarily increase diversity by injecting more token-level randomness, which may introduce step-wise noise and lead to incoherent trajectories. We uncover that smaller models within the same model family inherently exhibit higher policy-level diversity, indicated by their superior pass@k relative to larger counterparts as sample counts increase. Unlike token-level noise, this diversity is temporally correlated, preserves logical consistency, and provides structured exploration signals for gradient estimation. We thus propose S2L-PO (Small-to-Large Policy Optimization), a framework that leverages fixed small models as natural explorers to train larger models. To balance exploration and exploitation, we design a progressive annealing strategy that transitions from offline small-model rollouts to the large learner's own sampling. This shift elegantly avoids mid-training performance drops caused by the small model's capacity limits, achieving faster convergence and unlocking a higher performance ceiling. S2L-PO improves accuracy on diverse mathematical reasoning benchmarks (e.g., +8.8% on AIME 24 using a 1.7B explorer to guide the 8B model) while reducing rollout compute.

</details>


### [134] [Beyond Accuracy: Evaluating Efficiency, Robustness and Explainability in Deep Learning for Malaria Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30734)
*Olivier Kanamugire,Kerol Djoumessi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究评估了四种深度学习模型在疟疾诊断中的表现，发现轻量级模型在预测性能上与重型模型相当，且CAM类可解释性方法效果更好，但在图像受损时解释可靠性会显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 疟疾是撒哈拉以南非洲地区的主要死因之一，但当地诊断基础设施匮乏，及时准确的诊断极具挑战。尽管深度学习为自动化疟疾筛查提供了可能，但计算成本高和决策过程不透明阻碍了其临床应用，因此需要系统评估不同架构模型的性能、鲁棒性和可解释性。

Method: 在NLM-Malaria数据集上对四种不同架构和参数规模的深度学习模型进行基准测试，综合评估预测性能、鲁棒性和事后可解释性。采用Friedman检验分析性能差异，使用CAM类和细粒度归因类XAI方法进行解释，并通过三种图像损坏类型测试模型鲁棒性。

Result: 轻量级高效模型在预测性能上与重型模型相当，Friedman检验显示无统计显著性差异。CAM类XAI方法能一致地定位诊断相关区域，而细粒度归因方法产生的解释针对性较弱，尤其在重型骨干网络上。在图像损坏条件下，模型置信度下降速度快于准确率，可作为人工审查的信号。然而，所有XAI方法对损坏都不鲁棒，在临床实践中可能出现的噪声水平下解释可靠性就会下降，即使预测仍保持准确。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持在资源受限环境中部署轻量级架构进行疟疾诊断，但同时强调了事后解释的脆弱性是临床负责任部署的重要考量因素。这为疟疾自动诊断系统的实际应用提供了重要指导，建议在临床部署时关注XAI方法的局限性。

Abstract: Malaria remains a leading cause of mortality in sub-Saharan Africa, where scarce diagnostic infrastructure makes timely, accurate diagnosis particularly challenging. While deep learning offers a compelling path toward automated malaria screening, clinical adoption is hindered by computational cost and opacity in decision-making. This work benchmarks four deep learning models spanning a wide range of designed design architectures and model capacities on the NLM-Malaria dataset, jointly evaluating predictive performance, robustness, and post-hoc explainability. We find that lightweight, efficient-by-design models match their heavier counterparts in predictive performance, and the Friedman test confirms no statistically significant performance differences. CAM-based XAI methods consistently localize diagnostically relevant regions, while fine-grained attribution methods produce less targeted explanations, particularly with heavier backbones. Robustness evaluation under three types of image corruption further reveals that model confidence degrades faster than accuracy, providing a practical signal for human review. However, no XAI method is robust to corruption, with explanation reliability degrading at noise levels plausible in clinical practice, even when predictions remain accurate. These findings support the deployment of lightweight architectures for malaria diagnosis in resource-constrained settings, while highlighting the vulnerability of post-hoc explanations as an important consideration for responsible clinical deployment.

</details>


### [135] [Unlearning in Diffusion Models: A Unified Framework with KL Divergence and Likelihood Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30825)
*Shervin Khalafi,Alejandro Ribeiro,Dongsheng Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于约束优化的扩散模型遗忘框架，通过KL散度或似然约束实现遗忘目标，在保持模型效用的同时有效移除不良数据或概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型中的遗忘面临两个根本冲突的目标：移除不良数据或概念，同时保持预训练模型的效用。现有方法难以有效平衡这一保留-遗忘权衡，需要一种原则性的优化框架来显式建模这种约束关系。

Method: 提出一个约束优化框架，将遗忘问题建模为最小化与预训练模型的偏差，同时满足与遗忘分布的显式分离约束。具体构建了三个优化问题：基于反向KL散度、前向KL散度和似然约束的约束优化问题，建立了强对偶性理论，并开发了原始-对偶算法求解。

Result: 实验表明，基于KL约束的方法在概念和数据遗忘任务中，相比基于权重的基线方法实现了更优的保留-遗忘权衡。基于似然的方法在达到同等遗忘效果的同时，相比基线更好地保留了需要保留的概念。

Conclusion: 所提出的约束优化框架为扩散模型遗忘提供了一种原则性方法，通过理论分析和实验验证，证明了该方法能够有效解决保留效用与遗忘目标之间的冲突，在概念和数据遗忘任务中均表现出优越性能。

Abstract: Unlearning in diffusion models aims to remove undesirable data or concepts while preserving the utility of pretrained models -- two fundamentally conflicting objectives. We propose a principled constrained optimization framework that formulates unlearning as minimizing the deviation from a pretrained model, subject to explicit separation constraints from the unlearning distributions. Specifically, we formulate three constrained optimization problems based on reverse and forward KL divergences, and likelihood constraints. The first two generalize existing approaches for concept and data unlearning, while the third offers a novel and natural formulation for unlearning. Despite the nonconvexity of the KL constraints, we establish strong duality for all three problems, enabling us to explicitly characterize their optimal solutions as unlearning targets and develop primal-dual algorithms for each formulation. Experimental results demonstrate that our KL-constrained approach achieves superior retention-unlearning tradeoffs compared to weight-based baselines for concept and data unlearning, and that our likelihood-based approach matches unlearning effectiveness while better preserving retained concepts compared to baselines.

</details>


### [136] [FLAG: Flow Policy MaxEnt-RL by Latent Augmented Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30749)
*Sungha Kim,Gawon Lee,Jusuk Lee,Jonghae Park,H. Jin Kim,Daesol Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FLAG方法，通过引入流潜变量增强指导，解决了最大熵强化学习中生成式策略的重要性权重崩溃问题，实现了高维动作空间下的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大熵强化学习（MaxEnt-RL）虽然能够实现鲁棒的探索，但实际实现通常将策略限制为简单的高斯分布。近期方法通过重要性加权监督学习引入表达性强的生成式策略，但容易发生重要性权重崩溃（importance weight collapse），限制了其在高维动作空间中的可扩展性。

Method: 提出FLAG（Flow policy with Latent-Augmented Guidance）方法：通过定位采样区域来避免在整个动作空间上进行重要性采样所导致的权重退化问题。具体而言，FLAG将状态空间与流潜变量（flow latent variable）相结合，并优化一个可证明一致的最大熵强化学习代理目标。

Result: 实验表明，FLAG能够在有限的重要性样本下实现表达性策略优化，并能扩展至高维控制任务。在多个挑战性基准测试中，FLAG达到了最先进的性能表现。

Conclusion: 通过局部化采样区域和潜变量增强，FLAG成功缓解了重要性权重崩溃问题，为高维动作空间中的表达性策略优化提供了有效的解决方案，在多个基准测试中取得了领先性能。

Abstract: Maximum entropy reinforcement learning (MaxEnt-RL) enables robust exploration, yet practical implementations often restrict policies to simple Gaussians.
  While recent approaches incorporate expressive generative policies via importance-weighted supervised learning, they are prone to importance weight collapse, which limits their scalability in high-dimensional action spaces.
  Our key insight is to mitigate this limitation by localizing the sampling region, avoiding the weight degeneracy induced by importance sampling over the entire action space.
  To instantiate this insight, we introduce \textbf{FLAG} (\textbf{F}low policy with \textbf{L}atent-\textbf{A}ugmented \textbf{G}uidance).
  FLAG augments the state space with a flow latent variable and optimizes a provably consistent proxy MaxEnt-RL objective.
  We empirically demonstrate that FLAG enables expressive policy optimization with limited importance samples and scales to high-dimensional control tasks.
  Furthermore, FLAG achieves state-of-the-art performance across challenging benchmarks. Our project webpage: https://flag-rl.github.io/

</details>


### [137] [Chain-of-Thought and Compressed Looped Transformers: A Memory-Budget Separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30757)
*Haozhou Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较了链式思维和循环Transformer的记忆机制差异，证明压缩循环受限于其循环状态大小，无法解决P完全问题，而链式思维可以。记忆预算是测试时推理的关键资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 链式思维提示和循环Transformer都为固定模型提供测试时计算能力，但它们的记忆机制不同：链式思维将中间状态存储在上下文中的生成tokens中，而循环Transformer通过循环隐藏激活传递状态。作者认为这种持久的可变记忆是测试时推理的核心资源，需要系统研究不同记忆机制的能力边界。

Method: 比较三种记忆机制：(1)压缩潜在循环(compressed latent loop)，(2)完整序列状态循环(full sequence-state loop)，(3)链式思维暂存器。基于标准复杂性假设进行理论分析，并通过可控的指针追踪和联想召回实验进行实证验证。

Result: 压缩循环的能力受其循环状态大小限制：即使运行更多步骤，小循环状态仍只是小空间推理器。在标准复杂性假设下，这种循环无法解决在logspace归约下P完全的问题，而多项式长度的链式思维可以解决。完整序列状态循环携带所有输入位置的状态，处于记忆丰富的机制中，接近显式暂存器。

Conclusion: 记忆预算视角解释了不同记忆机制的能力差异：当持久状态预算与任务的工作记忆需求匹配时，性能表现良好。压缩循环的局限性在于无法创建增长的暂存空间，这为理解和设计测试时计算架构提供了重要洞见。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought prompting and looped Transformers both give a fixed model more test-time computation, but they differ in what they remember. Chain-of-thought stores intermediate state in generated tokens that remain in the context, whereas a looped Transformer carries state through recurrent hidden activations. We argue that this persistent mutable memory is a central resource for test-time reasoning.
  We compare three memory regimes, the compressed latent loop, the full sequence-state loop, and the chain-of-thought scratchpad. Our main result shows that a compressed loop is limited by the size of its recurrent state. Running the loop longer adds computation but does not by itself create a growing scratchpad, so a loop with a small recurrent state remains a small-space reasoner even when run for many steps. Under a standard complexity assumption, such loops cannot decide problems that are P-complete under logspace reductions, whereas polynomial-length chain-of-thought can.
  The separation is specific to compressed loops, as full sequence-state loops carry state at every input position and live in a memory-rich regime closer to explicit scratchpads. Controlled pointer-chasing and associative-recall sweeps illustrate this memory-budget view, with performance sensitive to whether the persistent-state budget matches the task's working-memory demand.

</details>


### [138] [DARTS: Distribution-Aware Active Rollout Trajectory Shaping for Accelerating LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30859)
*Yujie Wang,Siwei Chen,Longzan Luo,Xinyi Liu,Xupeng Miao,Fangcheng Fu,Bin Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对强化学习中因长尾响应长度分布导致的rollout效率瓶颈，提出主动分布塑造范式，通过分布感知轨迹采样和自适应冗余分配机制，在保证模型性能的前提下实现最高1.77倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作通过提示级尾调度缓解长尾影响，但未触及效率瓶颈的根本来源——分布本身。作者发现长尾分布中存在提示内长尾，且这些长尾通常包含无效冗余内容，这是导致系统开销的根本原因。

Method: 提出主动分布塑造范式，包含两个核心组件：(1) 分布感知轨迹采样机制，从冗余探索空间中为每个提示选择轨迹；(2) 自适应冗余分配方案，最大化分布塑造效果和系统效率。该方法直接塑造rollout分布，使其趋向简洁性和确定性。

Result: 实验表明，相比最先进系统实现最高1.77倍加速，且不损害模型性能。验证了主动分布塑造方法在解决长尾诱导开销方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过从根源上重塑rollout分布而非仅缓解其影响，可以更有效地解决强化学习中的效率瓶颈问题，为提升模型训练效率提供了新思路。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has become pivotal for improving model capabilities yet suffers from rollout efficiency bottlenecks due to the long-tail response length distribution. While existing works mitigate the impact of long tails via prompt-level tail scheduling, we focus on the root source of inefficiency: the distribution itself. Specifically, we characterize the long-tail distribution at a finer granularity, identifying intra-prompt long tails, and revealing that they frequently consist of ineffective verbosity. To address this, we propose a novel paradigm of active distribution shaping to shape the rollout distribution towards conciseness and certainty, thereby fundamentally resolving tail-induced overheads. We achieve this through a distribution-aware trajectory sampling mechanism, which selects trajectories from a redundant exploration space for each prompt, and an adaptive redundancy allocation scheme to maximize both shaping effectiveness and system efficiency. Experiments demonstrate significant acceleration over state-of-the-art systems by up to 1.77x without compromising model performance.

</details>


### [139] [Efficient and Uncertainty-Aware Diffusion Framework for Offline-to-Online Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30776)
*Ha Manh Bui,Metod Jazbec,Eric Nalisnick,Anqi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DUAL框架，利用扩散模型的先验知识和不确定性量化，解决离线到在线强化学习（O2O-RL）中的分布偏移问题，提升了在线阶段的数据效率与期望回报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的O2O-RL方法在离线与在线分布之间存在偏移，容易导致性能下降；现有方法通过在扩散模型采样的轨迹数据上微调策略来缓解该问题，但仍有提升空间。

Method: 提出DUAL框架：在离线阶段利用扩散模型的先验知识蒸馏出快速采样的扩散行动者策略和转移模型；在线阶段采用拉普拉斯近似和距离转移状态偏移检测，通过不确定性量化实现更优的探索与利用权衡。理论上证明了带有拉普拉斯近似的行动者损失可作为认知不确定性的合理估计代理。

Result: 在多种设置和环境下的实验表明，DUAL相比现有O2O-RL基线方法，能够获得更高的在线期望回报。

Conclusion: DUAL通过结合扩散模型先验与不确定性量化，有效缓解了O2O-RL中的分布偏移问题，在保证数据效率的同时显著提升了在线性能，为该领域提供了一种高效、理论支撑充分的新方法。

Abstract: Offline-to-Online Reinforcement Learning (O2O-RL) leverages an offline, pre-trained policy to minimize costly online interactions. Although data-efficient, O2O-RL is susceptible to shifts between offline and online distributions. Existing work aims to mitigate the harm of this shift by finetuning the policy on trajectory data sampled from a diffusion model. Inspired by this line of work, we propose DUAL: an efficient \textbf{D}iffusion \textbf{U}ncertainty-\textbf{A}ware framework for offline-to-online reinforcement \textbf{L}earning. DUAL utilizes the prior knowledge of the diffusion model to distill a fast-sampling diffusion actor policy and transition model in the offline phase. DUAL also employs a Laplace approximation and distance transition-state-shift detection, thereby using uncertainty quantification to improve exploration versus exploitation in the online phase. We formally show that our actor loss with the Laplace approximation provides a proxy for a principled estimate of epistemic uncertainty. Empirically, DUAL improves the online expected return over O2O-RL baselines across multiple settings and environments.

</details>


### [140] [AbstainGNN: Teaching Graph Neural Networks to Abstain for Graph Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30786)
*Xixun Lin,Zhiheng Zhou,Zhengyin Zhang,Yancheng Chen,Shuai Zhang,Ge Zhang,Shichao Zhu,Lixin Zou,Chuan Zhou,Peng Zhang,Shirui Pan,Yanan Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了AbstainGNN框架，使图神经网络能够在不确定性高时选择放弃预测而非做出错误决策，从PAC-Bayesian角度理论刻画了分类错误与拒绝代价的权衡，在基准数据集上取得优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图神经网络在图分类任务中被迫在高不确定性或未知条件下进行预测，导致不可靠的决策，这在安全关键场景中会产生严重影响。

Method: 提出AbstainGNN框架，显式建模预测函数和弃权函数，从PAC-Bayesian泛化视角理论刻画分类错误与拒绝代价的权衡，推导统一学习目标，并设计包含预测函数预热和弃权函数校准的两阶段训练策略。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，AbstainGNN在相同拒绝率下优于现有弃权方法，实现了更优的分类性能。

Conclusion: AbstainGNN为图分类提供了一种理论驱动的弃权机制，能够有效提升模型在高不确定性场景下的可靠性，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Graph classification is a core task in graph data mining with widespread real-world applications. Recent advances in graph neural networks (GNNs) have led to substantial performance improvements for graph classification. However, existing GNNs are typically forced to make predictions even under high uncertainty or unknown conditions, resulting in unreliable decisions that can severely impact downstream tasks, particularly in safety-critical scenarios. To address this critical limitation, we propose AbstainGNN, a novel and theory-driven framework for graph classification with abstention, which enables GNNs to reject uncertain predictions instead of producing incorrect decisions. Specifically, AbstainGNN explicitly models both the predictive function and the abstention function, allowing for effective utilization of graph structural information. Moreover, unlike existing heuristic abstention methods, we theoretically characterize the trade-off between classification errors and rejection costs from a PAC-Bayesian generalization perspective, and derive a unified learning objective for model optimization. Guided by this theoretical insight, we further develop an efficient two-stage training strategy consisting of predictive function warm-start and abstention function calibration. Extensive experiments on five benchmark datasets show that AbstainGNN outperforms existing abstention methods, achieving superior classification performance under the same rejection rates.

</details>


### [141] [Inverse Reinforcement Learning without an Optimal Demonstrator: A Feasible Reward Set Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30903)
*Kihyun Kim,Shripad Deshmukh,Nikos Vlassis,Jiawei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于可行奖励集的逆强化学习框架，用于处理来自多个不完美演示者（具有异质次优水平）的数据，通过线性约束编码每个演示者的次优水平并求交集，理论证明了可行集的单调收缩性并给出了真实最优奖励的恢复保证，同时提出了处理高维环境的离线算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的逆强化学习假设演示数据来自单一最优演示者，但在实际应用中，数据通常来自多个具有不同次优水平的不完美演示者，这促使研究者开发能够有效整合异质次优演示数据的奖励学习方法。

Method: 提出可行奖励集框架：为每个演示者将其声明的次优水平编码为线性约束，构建相应的可行集，然后对所有演示者的可行集求交集；提出了处理奖励模糊性的策略，并开发了适用于高维环境的带函数逼近的离线算法。

Result: 理论分析表明联合可行集随着数据增加单调收缩，给出了新演示者严格收紧可行集的精确刻画；建立了真实最优演示者可行奖励集的两个恢复保证：一个依赖于与最优占用测度的接近度，另一个仅需要足够的覆盖率而无需近似最优演示者；在表格网格世界和大语言模型微调场景中的实验验证了理论预测，并展示了框架相比基线方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架成功地将异质次优演示者的信息整合到统一的逆强化学习框架中，为多演示者场景下的奖励学习提供了理论保证和实用的算法，实验结果证明了方法的有效性和理论预测的一致性，为处理实际应用中的不完美演示数据提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) typically assumes demonstrations from a single optimal demonstrator, but in many applications data come from multiple imperfect demonstrators with heterogeneous suboptimality levels. We study reward learning in this setting through a feasible-reward-set framework: for each demonstrator, we encode its declared suboptimality level as a linear constraint and intersect the resulting feasible sets across demonstrators. Our theoretical analysis shows that the joint feasible set shrinks monotonically as data are added, and we give an exact characterization of when a new demonstrator strictly tightens it. We further establish two recovery guarantees for the feasible reward set of the ground-truth optimal demonstrator: one bound depends on closeness to the optimal occupancy, while the other requires only sufficient coverage and no near-optimal demonstrator. On the practical side, we introduce strategies to address the inherent reward ambiguity in the obtained reward set and provide an offline algorithm with function approximation for high-dimensional environments. Experiments in tabular grid-world and large language model (LLM) fine-tuning settings are consistent with the theoretical predictions and demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework over baselines.

</details>


### [142] [De-attribute to Forget for LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30919)
*Xinyang Lu,Jiabao Pan,Rachael Hwee Ling Sim,See-Kiong Ng,Anthony Kum Hoe Tung,Bryan Kian Hsiang Low*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DareU框架，这是首个基于数据归因奖励的LLM遗忘方法，通过强化学习降低模型响应在遗忘数据上的归因分数，在保证模型效用的同时实现了有效遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM遗忘方法通常依赖优化预测损失（如在遗忘集上最大化损失），但经常面临过度遗忘和模型效用降低等关键问题，需要一种新的优化目标来平衡遗忘质量和模型性能。

Method: 将LLM遗忘的优化目标重新定义为数据归因归零问题，提出首个基于数据归因奖励的遗忘框架DareU，采用强化学习更新LLM，通过降低其生成响应对遗忘数据所有者的归因分数（即去归因化）来实现遗忘。使用LLM分类器作为归因的高效近似进行实证评估。

Result: DareU在实证评估中优于现有基线方法，能够实现有效遗忘，同时在遗忘质量和模型效用之间取得良好平衡。

Conclusion: 基于数据归因奖励的遗忘框架为LLM遗忘提供了一种有效的新范式，能够解决传统损失优化方法面临的过度遗忘和效用降低问题。

Abstract: The rapid development of large language models (LLMs) has raised concerns on the use of inappropriate data for training, which has led to a growing interest in LLM unlearning. Many existing LLM unlearning approaches rely on optimizing prediction loss(es), such as maximizing the loss on the forget set, but often face critical issues like over-forgetting and poor model utility. To address them, this paper novelly frames the optimization objective for LLM unlearning as one of zeroing out data attribution instead. In particular, we propose the first LLM unlearning framework based on data attribution rewards called DareU that performs reinforcement learning to update the LLM by reducing the attribution score of its generated responses (i.e., de-attributing) to the forget data owners. Empirical evaluation using an LLM classifier as an efficient approximation of attribution shows that DareU outperforms existing baselines by achieving effective unlearning while balancing forget quality and model utility well.

</details>


### [143] [Conformal Reliability: A New Evaluation Metric for Conditional Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30807)
*Yachen Gao,Xinwei Sun,Yikai Wang,Ye Shi,Jingya Wang,Jianfeng Feng,Yanwei Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了基于共形预测的可靠性评分（Reliability Score）来评估条件生成模型，并设计了CReL框架来高效计算该指标，有效捕捉生成过程中的不确定性和风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的条件生成模型评估指标通常只评估单个输出，无法捕捉生成过程中的变异性或潜在风险，缺乏考虑模型固有不确定性的合适评估指标。

Method: 提出基于共形预测的可靠性评分指标，测量在预设置信水平下预测集合内的最坏情况性能；开发CReL框架来构建具有期望覆盖率的预测集，并在构建的预测集内准确优化可靠性评分，解决了高维输出空间和非凸性带来的计算挑战。

Result: 理论证明了预测集的覆盖率保证；实验表明该方法相比现有方法产生更具信息量的预测集；在合成数据、图像到文本和文本到图像任务上验证了指标的可解释性和计算框架的有效性。

Conclusion: CReL框架能够有效评估条件生成模型的可靠性，提供了具有理论保证的预测集构建方法，为生成模型的可靠性评估提供了新的视角和工具。

Abstract: Conditional generative models have recently achieved remarkable success in various applications. However, a suitable metric for evaluating the reliability of these models, which takes into account their inherent uncertainty, is still lacking. Existing metrics, which typically assess a single output, may fail to capture the variability or potential risks in generation. In this paper, we propose a novel evaluation metric called reliability score based on conformal prediction, which measures the worst-case performance within the prediction set at a pre-specified confidence level. However, computing this score is challenging due to the high-dimensional nature of the output space and the nonconvexity of both the metric function and the prediction set. To efficiently compute this score, we introduce Conformal ReLiability (CReL), a framework that can (i) construct the prediction set with desired coverage; and (ii) accurately optimize the reliability score within the constructed prediction set. We provide theoretical results on coverage and demonstrate empirically that our method produces more informative prediction sets than existing approaches. Experiments on synthetic data and the image-to-text and text-to-image tasks further demonstrate the interpretability of our new metric, and the validity and effectiveness of our computational framework. Source code can be found at https://ggc29.github.io/CReL/.

</details>


### [144] [DEM: A Distilled Explanation Model for Interpretable Anomaly Detection in Physiological Sensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31007)
*Jyotirmoy Singh,Anushka Roy,Shreea Bose,Chittaranjan Hota*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出蒸馏解释模型（DEM），这是一个三阶段“玻璃盒”框架，将梯度提升专家的非线性知识蒸馏到决策树中，在生理传感器异常检测中实现了高精度（AUC 0.9964）与内在可解释性的统一，推理速度比SHAP快1235倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线体域网（WBAN）生理传感器数据的异常检测（由传感器故障、网络中断或数据丢失引起）会导致误报，这既要求高预测精度，又要求具有临床可解释性。现有方法存在两难：黑盒模型性能强但缺乏透明度，事后解释方法（如SHAP、LIME）并非预测本身。因此，亟需一种既能保证性能又能提供可信、内在解释的模型。

Method: 提出蒸馏解释模型（DEM），包含三个阶段：1）建立线性基线模型；2）训练梯度提升专家（XGBoost）拟合残差；3）将专家模型的非线性知识蒸馏为决策树，该树直接对相对线性基线的残差进行操作。关键创新在于解释树不是对预测的近似，而是预测本身，并引入蒸馏保真度指标量化解释树对专家模型非线性贡献的捕捉程度。

Result: 在四个生理数据集（MIMIC-IV、WESAD、eICU和自建SmartNet WBAN语料库）上进行评估，DEM在临床情境异常检测上达到AUC 0.9964，在可穿戴压力检测上达到AUC 0.9047。每1000个样本推理仅需0.17毫秒，比基于SHAP的事后解释快1235倍。消融实验证实XGBoost蒸馏步骤相比朴素残差拟合有显著提升，深度敏感性分析展示了用户可控的精度-可解释性权衡。

Conclusion: DEM成功地将高性能与内在可解释性相结合，通过生成人类可读的if-then规则并提供可控的深度，解决了现有方法中性能与可解释性的权衡困境，适用于实时生理监测场景，为临床可信的异常检测提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in physiological sensor data from Wireless Body Area Networks (WBANs) can be caused by sensor faults, network disruptions, or missing data, leading to false alarms. Hence, it demands both high predictive accuracy and clinically interpretable explanations. Existing approaches rely either on black-box models that achieve strong performance but offer no transparency, or on post-prediction explanation methods such as SHAP and LIME. In this paper, we propose the Distilled Explanation Model (DEM), a three-stage glass-box framework that distills the non-linear knowledge of a gradient boosting expert into an interpretable decision tree operating on residuals relative to a linear baseline, so that the explanation is not an approximation but the prediction itself. DEM introduces a novel distillation fidelity metric that quantifies how faithfully the explanation tree captures the expert model's non-linear contribution, providing a principled measure of explanation trustworthiness absent from prior interpretable models. Evaluated across four physiological datasets, including MIMIC-IV, WESAD, eICU, and an in-house SmartNet WBAN corpus, DEM achieves an AUC of 0.9964 on clinical contextual anomaly detection and 0.9047 on wearable stress detection while producing human-readable if-then rules at a controllable depth. Inference requires 0.17ms per 1000 samples, rendering DEM 1235x faster than SHAP-based post-hoc explanation and suitable for real-time physiological monitoring. Ablation studies confirm that the XGBoost distillation step provides measurable gains over naive residual fitting, and depth-sensitivity analysis demonstrates an explicit, user-controlled accuracy-interpretability trade-off unique to DEM among existing intrinsically interpretable models.

</details>


### [145] [IRIS: time-structured manifold projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30810)
*Brian Ondov,Chia-Hsuan Chang,Weipeng Zhou,Xingjian Zhang,Xueqing Peng,Yutong Xie,Huan He,Qiaozhu Mei,Hua Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了IRIS算法，一种能够同时保持时间顺序和流形拓扑结构的新型流形学习可视化方法，解决了t-SNE和UMAP等现有算法无法处理时间动态性的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维生物医学数据（如细胞-基因矩阵）越来越多地以时间序列形式产生，但现有的流形学习算法（如t-SNE和UMAP）无法在布局中整合时间顺序信息，导致细胞类型或其他类别的动态变化被遮蔽。

Method: 提出IRIS算法，这是一种新型的流形学习算法，能够在布局中同时按时间顺序和流形拓扑结构进行组织，从而保留数据的动态特征。

Result: IRIS能够可视化广泛的动态生物医学数据，包括单细胞RNA测序(scRNA-seq)、比较宏基因组和文献数据。

Conclusion: IRIS填补了现有流形学习算法在处理时序高维生物医学数据方面的空白，为动态生物过程的分析和可视化提供了有效工具。

Abstract: High-dimensional biomedical data, such as cell-by-gene matrices, are increasingly generated temporally. However, Manifold Learning algorithms, like t-SNE and UMAP, cannot incorporate time-ordering in their layouts, obfuscating the dynamics of cell types or other classes. As a solution, we present IRIS, a new Manifold Learning algorithm that structures layouts both chronologically and by manifold topology. IRIS can visualize a wide range of dynamic biomedical data, including scRNA-seq, comparative metagenomics, and literature.

</details>


### [146] [Annealed Softmax Greedy in Many-Armed Bayesian Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31034)
*William Overman,Mohsen Bayati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在具有大量近优臂的贝叶斯伯努利老虎机中，退火Softmax策略可实现近最优的O(√T)贝叶斯遗憾，为RLVR等不确定性无关方法的有效性提供了理论解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习可验证奖励（RLVR）和GRPO等方法在更新策略时不显式追踪认知不确定性，但仍表现出良好效果。本文旨在为这种不确定性无关更新的有效性提供理论解释。

Method: 研究多臂贝叶斯伯努利老虎机中的退火Softmax（玻尔兹曼）策略，该策略根据经验均值奖励的Softmax选择动作。分析假设先验分布满足线性上尾条件（β正则性，β=1情形），意味着存在大量近优臂。理论推导该策略的贝叶斯遗憾界。

Result: 在β正则性条件下，退火Softmax贪婪策略实现贝叶斯遗憾Õ(m+T/m)，当臂数m=Θ(√T)时达到Õ(√T)的近最优速率。其机制是：大量臂保持接近最优的经验均值，当Softmax采样非经验最优臂时，往往采样到另一个近优臂而非劣质臂。反之，在臂数较少时，相同策略可能遭受线性遗憾。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了RLVR方法的结构类比：具有不可忽略正确完成概率的基础策略扮演β正则性角色。在存在大量近优解的场景下，即使不显式建模不确定性，概率性探索策略也能有效工作，因为探索往往导向近优解。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) and group-based policy optimization methods such as GRPO update a stochastic policy by sampling multiple completions per prompt and increasing the policy's probability on those with higher reward, regularized by a KL penalty toward a reference policy. These updates do not include explicit mechanisms that track epistemic uncertainty. This paper studies a stylized explanation for why such uncertainty-agnostic updates can nevertheless be effective. We analyze an annealed softmax (Boltzmann) policy that selects actions according to a softmax of empirical mean rewards in a many-armed Bayesian Bernoulli bandit. Under a linear upper-tail condition on the prior (the $β=1$ case of $β$-regularity), which implies an abundance of near-optimal arms, we prove that annealed softmax greedy achieves Bayes regret $\tilde{O}(m + T/m)$, and in particular $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$ when the number of arms scales as $m = Θ(\sqrt{T})$. This is the near-optimal Bayes regret rate in this regime, attained also by empirical-mean greedy. Under $β$-regularity, many arms maintain empirical means close to the optimum throughout learning, so when softmax samples an arm other than the empirically best, that arm tends to be another near-optimal one rather than a clearly inferior one. By contrast, with a small number of arms, the same kind of softmax policy can suffer linear regret. The result also provides a structural analogy to RLVR, where a base policy with a non-negligible probability of producing a correct completion plays the role of $β$-regularity.

</details>


### [147] [Non-destructive Identification of Oyster Species is possible from Hyperspectral Images with Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30811)
*Ethan Kane Waters,Max Wingfield,Aiden Mellor,Paul Stewart,Iman Tahmasbian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究开发了基于高光谱成像（HSI）的非破坏性快速方法，用于区分黑唇岩牡蛎（BL）和悉尼岩牡蛎（SR），PLS-DA模型分类准确率达100%，优于CNN模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统牡蛎物种鉴别方法（如DNA分析）具有破坏性且耗时，无法满足商业品种开发和海鲜供应链溯源的需求，因此需要开发快速、非破坏性的鉴别技术。

Method: 使用高光谱成像相机（950-2515nm）扫描156个活体BL和SR牡蛎样本，采集左右壳的光谱反射率数据。采用偏最小二乘判别分析（PLS-DA）和卷积神经网络（CNN）结合蒙特卡洛交叉验证建立分类模型。同时使用电子显微镜分析贝壳表面和横截面的元素与矿物组成。

Result: PLS-DA模型从左右壳光谱数据中成功区分两个物种，测试集分类准确率中位数均达100%，优于CNN模型（右壳96%，左壳83%）。电子显微镜分析显示BL右壳有4层结构，而SR仅有2层。右壳外层元素组成存在差异：BL富含碳，SR富含氧，可能反映几丁质和糖蛋白的相对丰度或组成差异。模型导出的波长重要性对应这些化合物的特征官能团振动模式。透射分析表明光线可穿透贝壳边缘，光谱信号可能受对侧贝壳或肉质部分影响。

Conclusion: 高光谱成像技术结合PLS-DA模型为牡蛎物种鉴别提供了一种有效的快速、非破坏性方法，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Differentiating between oyster species is important for developing new commercial oyster species suited to production systems and is critical for traceability in seafood supply chains. Common methods, such as DNA profiling, are destructive and time consuming. The possibility of using hyperspectral imaging (HSI) for discriminating between Black-Lip rock (BL) and Sydney rock (SR) oysters was investigated. Live BL and SR samples (N = 156) were scanned with a HSI camera (950-2515nm). Partial Least Square Discriminant Analysis and Convolutional Neural Networks were trained with Monte Carlo Cross Validation to distinguish BL and SR oysters from the spectral reflectance of their left and rights valves. The PLS-DA model successfully distinguished between the species from both the left and right valves with a median test set classification accuracy of 100%, out performing the CNN with 83% and 96% respectively. Elemental and mineralogical composition in the surface and cross-section of oyster valves were measured with electron microscopy. Analysis of the right valve revealed a greater number of layers in BL compared to SR (4 vs 2). The concentrations of carbon and oxygen varied in the outer layer of the right valves, with BL being rich in carbon and SR being rich in oxygen. The variation in carbon and oxygen concentrations observed between BL and SR right valves may reflect differences in the relative abundance or composition of chitin and glycoproteins. This is supported by model-derived wavelength importance corresponding to vibrational modes of functional groups characteristic of these compounds. Transmittance analysis revealed that light was transmitted through the valves, around the valve edges, indicating that the spectral signatures may have been influenced by the other valve or the meat. Ultimately, the findings highlight an effective rapid, non-destructive methodology for oyster species.

</details>


### [148] [Learning to Solve and Optimize by Evolving Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31049)
*Veronika Semmelrock,Benedetta Strizzolo,Francesco Zuccato,Gerhard Friedrich,Patrick Rodler,Konstantin Schekotihin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了工具CHECKMATE，通过代码演化自动生成组合优化问题的求解算法，仅依赖问题的形式化规范（what）而无需专家设计求解过程（how），在配置和调度领域的实验中超越了最先进的求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的工业AI组合优化问题求解需要专家进行问题形式化、选择专用求解器并设计启发式方法，这要求专家不仅要定义“解是什么”，还要详细说明“如何求解”，过程复杂且成本高。

Method: 引入CHECKMATE工具，采用代码演化范式：利用形式化规范保证解的正确性并系统评估生成程序的性能，同时结合自然语言描述引导演化过程，从而自动生成优化算法。

Result: 在配置和调度两个工业领域的多个代表性问题上进行验证，演化生成的算法在所有案例中均持续超越了当前最先进的求解器。

Conclusion: 形式化方法在引导代码演化、自动求解复杂现实问题方面具有巨大潜力，标志着一个仅需定义问题规范而无需人为设计求解过程的范式转变。

Abstract: Combinatorial and optimization problems are fundamental to many industrial AI applications. Solving large-scale real-world instances of such problems typically requires careful problem formalization, specialized solvers, and expert-designed heuristics. Thus, experts need to specify not only what solutions are, but also how they are derived. By introducing the tool CHECKMATE, we show that algorithm generation via code evolution represents a paradigm shift by eliminating the need to formulate the how. CHECKMATE solely relies on the what. Specifically, a formal specification ensures solutions' correctness and enables systematic performance evaluation of the generated programs, while a natural language description guides the evolutionary process. The effectiveness of our method is demonstrated on selected problems from two industrial domains: configuration and scheduling. In all cases, the evolved algorithms consistently outperform state-of-the-art solvers. This underscores the potential of formal methods in guiding code evolution for automatically solving complex real-world problems.

</details>


### [149] [Learning Permutation-invariant Macroscopic Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30812)
*Zhichao Han,Mengyi Chen,Qianxiao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种置换不变的自动编码器框架，用于从无序的高维微观系统中学习宏观动力学，通过重建质量分布而非逐点重建来解决微观状态无序的问题，在粒子系统、流体和聚合物等多个场景中验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数据驱动方法通常使用自动编码器学习低维潜在状态，但假设输入中微观自由度具有固定顺序。然而在许多场景（如粒子系统）中，微观状态本质上是无序的，这促使作者开发一种能够学习置换不变潜在表示的自动编码器框架。

Method: 采用置换不变的编码器结构，设计解码器重建以观测点为中心的质量分布而非传统的逐样本重建，然后联合学习可观测量的宏观动力学与潜在状态。

Result: 在多种微观场景中展示了方法的有效性和鲁棒性，包括：学习相互作用粒子系统中的能量动力学、预测Lennard-Jones流体的混合动力学，以及根据聚合物在拉伸力场中运动的视频数据建模其拉伸动力学。

Conclusion: 所提出的置换不变自动编码器框架成功解决了微观状态无序情况下的宏观动力学建模问题，为高维微观系统的降维和动力学学习提供了一种有效且鲁棒的方法。

Abstract: Accurately modeling the macroscopic dynamics of high-dimensional microscopic systems is of broad interest across the sciences. Many data-driven approaches learn a low-dimensional latent state through an autoencoder trained for pointwise input reconstruction. These methods typically assume a fixed ordering of microscopic degrees of freedom in the input. However, in many settings, such as particle systems, the microscopic state is inherently unordered. This motivates an autoencoder framework that learns permutation-invariant latent representations. To this end, we adopt a permutation-invariant encoder and design the decoder to reconstruct the mass distribution centered at the observed points rather than per-sample reconstruction. We then jointly learn the macroscopic dynamics of the observables together with the latent states. We demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of the proposed method across a range of microscopic settings, including learning the energy dynamics in interacting particle systems, predicting mixing dynamics in Lennard-Jones fluids, and modeling the stretching dynamics from video data of polymers moving in an elongational force field.

</details>


### [150] [STEP: Learning STructured Embeddings for Progressive Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31061)
*Lucas Thil,Jesse Read,Rim Kaddah,Guillaume Doquet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对渐进式时间序列（如设备退化、任务完成等不可逆状态转换）的可解释表示学习方法，通过自监督对比学习构建低维潜在空间，从潜在向量的极坐标（θ, r）中提取"潜在指南针"，θ追踪状态进展，r识别活跃模式，在多个领域验证了该方法在端态预测、多步预测和可解释阶段分离方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理渐进式时间序列（不可逆状态转换）时缺乏可解释性，难以理解潜在状态的演变机制。需要一种既能保持预测性能又能提供透明解释的表示学习方法，使潜在状态的几何结构本身成为解释工具。

Method: 采用自监督对比学习目标，学习低维潜在空间，其几何结构具有可解释性：每个观测点成为流形上的点，该流形由两个固定的正交原型向量锚定。从该结构中提取潜在向量的极坐标（θ, r）形成"潜在指南针"，其中θ追踪底层状态的进展（如从健康到故障），r识别活跃模式（如运行条件），无需代理标签。在潜在指南针坐标上使用简单的线性回归器进行预测。

Result: 在工业退化、机器人任务和神经活动等多个领域上评估，该方法在端态预测、多步预测和可解释阶段分离三个关键能力上匹配或优于黑盒方法。基于潜在指南针坐标的简单线性回归器与深度架构具有竞争力，直接证明了底层状态以几何可访问的形式编码。

Conclusion: 该方法成功实现了渐进式时间序列的可解释表示学习，潜在空间的几何结构本身提供了对底层机制的解释。通过潜在指南针实现了状态进展和模式的分离，证明了透明性不需要牺牲预测性能，简单模型在良好表示上可以匹敌复杂黑盒模型。

Abstract: We present a novel method for learning interpretable representations of progressive time series, that is, data capturing irreversible state transitions such as degradation or task completion. Our approach uses a self-supervised contrastive objective to learn a low-dimensional latent space whose geometry is itself the interpretation: each observation becomes a point on a manifold anchored between two fixed orthogonal prototype vectors, and a trajectory becomes a path across that manifold. From this structure we read a latent compass, the polar coordinates (θ, r) of the latent vector, in which θ tracks the progression of the underlying state (e.g., from healthy to failed) and r identifies the active mode (e.g., the operating condition), without any proxy labels. We evaluate the approach against the state of the art on diverse domains, including industrial degradation, robotic tasks, and neural activity, validating three key capabilities: (1) end-state prediction, (2) multi-step forecasting, and (3) interpretable phase separation. Our method matches or improves over black-box counterparts on all of these while providing transparency about the underlying mechanisms. A simple linear regressor on top of the latent compass coordinates is competitive with deep architectures, direct quantitative evidence that the underlying state is encoded in a geometrically accessible form.

</details>


### [151] [Trust-Region Behavior Blending for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31159)
*Daniil Plyusov,Alexey Gorbatovski,Alexey Malakhov,Nikita Balagansky,Boris Shaposhnikov,Daria Korotyshova,Daniil Gavrilov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Trust-Region behavior Blending (TRB)方法，通过在KL信任区域内用最接近教师的行为策略替换早期学生策略，有效解决了在线策略蒸馏中早期rollout质量差的问题，在数学推理蒸馏任务上取得了最佳平均表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏(OPD)虽然解决了离线蒸馏的前缀不匹配问题，但早期学生策略生成的rollout质量较差，导致教师监督作用于低质量前缀，影响蒸馏效果。

Method: 提出Trust-Region behavior Blending (TRB)预热方法，在以学生为中心的KL信任区域内，用最接近教师的行为策略替换早期rollout策略，同时保持每个前缀的反向KL OPD损失不变；KL预算逐渐退火至零，使训练在预热后回归纯学生rollout。

Result: 在两个数学推理蒸馏设置中，TRB在所有对比方法中取得了最强的平均性能表现。

Conclusion: TRB方法通过信任区域行为混合有效改善了在线策略蒸馏的早期训练质量，验证了该方法在知识蒸馏任务中的有效性。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on prefixes sampled from its own policy while matching a stronger teacher. This addresses the prefix mismatch of offline distillation, but early student rollouts can still be poor, placing teacher supervision on weak or low-quality prefixes. We propose Trust-Region behavior Blending (TRB), a warmup method that replaces the early rollout policy with the closest-to-teacher behavior policy inside a student-centered KL trust region, while keeping the per-prefix reverse-KL OPD loss unchanged. The KL budget is annealed to zero, so training returns to pure student rollouts after warmup. Across two math-reasoning distillation settings, TRB attains the strongest average among the compared methods.

</details>


### [152] [Cross-Layer Subspace Coupling for LLM Compression: A Unifying Framework and Its Empirical Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30836)
*Snigdha Chandan Khilar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文统一了现有的SVD压缩方法，发现虽然显著降低了权重重构误差，但在实际任务中表现不佳，揭示了权重空间重构目标在跨层压缩中的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期针对大语言模型的SVD压缩方法（如SVD LLM和Basis Sharing）缺乏统一的理论框架。研究者希望将这些方法归纳到同一优化问题下，并验证联合跨层优化是否能提升压缩效果。

Method: 将现有SVD压缩方法统一到一个优化框架中，并通过数学证明和在Pythia模型上的实验验证该方法对权重重构误差的改善效果，同时分析其在下游任务中的表现差异及失效机理。

Result: 统一方法将权重重构误差降低了最高46%，但在实际下游任务中表现严重退化。困惑度和准确率等指标相比标准的单层SVD LLM方法显著恶化。失效原因在于：尽管bundle方法在数学上耦合了相邻层，但Transformer的残差流在前向传播过程中实际解耦了这些层。

Conclusion: 权重空间重构是跨层压缩的错误目标，单层优化的重要性超过了联合跨层优化。未来的压缩方法应聚焦于单层激活重构而非权重空间重构。

Abstract: Recent SVD based compression methods for large language models like SVD LLM and Basis Sharing can be unified under one optimization problem. While mathematical proofs and tests on Pythia models show this unified approach improves weight reconstruction error by up to 46% percent it fails in practical tasks. Downstream metrics like perplexity and accuracy severely degrade compared to standard per layer SVD LLM. The authors explain this failure mechanistically. Although the bundle method mathematically couples adjacent layers the transformer residual stream actually decouples them during forward passes. Thus per layer optimality matters more than joint cross layer optimization. The paper concludes that weight space reconstruction is a flawed objective for cross layer compression and future methods must focus on per layer activation reconstruction instead.

</details>


### [153] [What changes after deployment? A survey on On-device Learning in TinyML](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31226)
*Massimo Pavan,Luca Pezzarossa,Fabrizio Pittorino,Manuel Roveri,Xenofon Fafoutis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了约70篇边缘设备学习（ODL）文献，提出以“分布变化机制”为统一原则进行分析，指出不同类型的分布变化需要不同解决方案，并揭示了方法基准与实际部署场景间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微控制器级设备上的机器学习模型在部署后面临分布变化导致静态模型性能下降的根本性挑战。现有文献缺乏对分布变化发生方式及其类型与解决方案匹配关系的系统刻画，亟需统一框架进行分析。

Method: 以“分布变化机制”作为统一原则，对约70篇边缘设备学习相关工作进行系统综述。分析不同分布变化类型如何影响可解决的设备应用场景、硬件平台选择以及解决方案结构设计。

Result: 系统梳理了分布变化类型与应用可解性、硬件平台、解决方案结构之间的对应关系。发现并指出方法基准与真实世界部署场景之间存在持续差距，该差距在不同分布变化类型下表现各异。

Conclusion: 不同分布变化类型需要针对性解决方案，现有方法基准与实际部署场景间存在显著鸿沟。未来研究需要更贴近真实世界分布变化场景的基准测试和评估方法。

Abstract: Machine learning models on microcontroller-class devices (TinyML) face a fundamental challenge: post-deployment distribution change undermines static models. On-device learning (ODL) addresses this by running the learning process directly on the device. The existing literature has not characterized how distribution change occurs or how different change types require different solutions. Approximately 70 ODL works are surveyed under one principle: the distribution change regime. The survey analyzes how different types of distribution change influence the applications addressable on-device, the hardware employed, and the structure of the solutions. A persistent gap between methodological benchmarks and real-world deployment scenarios is also identified.

</details>


### [154] [CoMem: Context Management with A Decoupled Long-Context Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30842)
*Yuwei Zhang,Chengyu Dong,Shuowei Jin,Changlong Yu,Hejie Cui,Hongye Jin,Xinyang Zhang,Hamed Bonab,Colin Lockard,Jianshu Chen,Zhenyu Shi,Jingbo Shang,Xian Li,Bing Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CoMem框架，通过将记忆管理与智能体工作流解耦并行执行，在保持性能的同时将延迟降低了1.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的上下文管理方法通过迭代摘要历史交互来解决长时域任务，但摘要过程会产生大量解码开销，显著影响部署时的端到端响应延迟。

Method: 提出CoMem框架，将记忆管理与主智能体工作流解耦并行执行；设计k步异步流水线，使记忆模型的摘要与智能体推理重叠以掩盖上下文处理延迟；引入奖励驱动的训练策略，确保记忆模型捕获充分的统计信息以支持智能体决策。

Result: 在SWE-Bench-Verified上的实验表明，相比普通长上下文解决方案，CoMem实现了1.4倍的延迟提升，同时保持了大部分性能；延迟增益随系统吞吐量增加而良好扩展。

Conclusion: CoMem提供了更优的效率-效果权衡，为独立优化智能体推理和记忆压缩提供了模块化路径。

Abstract: Context management enables agentic models to solve long-horizon tasks through iterative summarization of previous interaction histories. However, this process typically incurs substantial decoding overhead for the extra summarization tokens, which significantly affect the end-to-end response latency at deployment.
